bh-refute: KMS gives fp residually finite groups of every recursive WP complexity, so BH for fp RF groups already forces fp simple groups beyond every recursive bound; the complexity test for a universal envelope is passed on the input side, and only a host absorbing the KMS groups (e.g. an fp self-similar overgroup) is missing — fp-rf-groups-with-non-primitive-recursive-word-problem (verified)
- [bh-free-25, G2] deterministic-time-lifts-inherit-quantum-rigidity: a time-deterministic coding (automaton, self-similar, space-time) is automatically rigid against time walls and adds nothing against space walls. So G2 over a product reduces to a Lambda_2-free quantum-rigid row SFT whose automorphism group contains a jointly free-minimal Lambda_1. The space factor cannot be free (Piantadosi), and linear invertible time evolution over trees is a shift (trivial units). Over F2xF2 rigidity must come from a coupled rule.
- bh-g2-buildings: thickness kills two-sided rigidity. Every half-apartment of a thick building lies in ≥2 apartments, so flat/apartment subshifts over Z^d (Mozes product-of-trees tilings included, zero entropy or not) branch independently on both sides of every thick wall and are never quantum rigid. Building link closure proves reachability (irreducibility), never uniqueness; the same branching is compression only in one-sided (tail-equivalence) codings. So G2 cannot be supplied by building geometry over a translation lattice; the open building candidate is the lattice's own boundary shift Γ ↷ Ω (minimal, topologically free, NOT free) — thick-building-flat-sfts-are-never-quantum-rigid, euclidean-building-boundary-shifts-are-quantum-rigid (c1df2182b)
bh-free-58: Full-group hosts with torsion-free fixed-point germs and H_0-trivial comparison (CT_P(Z), V) are finite-subgroup homogeneous: an embedding of a finite group is determined up to conjugacy by its set of point stabilizers (clopen fixed sets + clopen transversals + equivalence of clopens). So stable letters over finite subgroups always exist in the host, and hard embedding and decision questions live only in infinite-order structure — ct-z-finite-subgroups-are-classified-by-stabilizer-sets
bh-free-54: Transvections are single carets: GL_{m+1}(Z) lies in the group of the ONE-generator edge-split operad on the rational Cantorization (transvections-are-single-carets-of-the-edge-split-operad). So BH for linear groups via Cantor hosts is Ore's condition for one positive monoid (edge-split-operad-satisfies-ore). Property (T) of SL_3(Z) forces that monoid to be non-tree (no Farley-cubical host), so the gate has the same shape as for nV: the positive monoid needs the lattice property that dyadic bricks enjoy.
- bh-verify-master: the Kazhdan wall (proper lengths: bounded sets small) and the distortion wall (zero sets of stable lengths small) are distinct; hosts can break one without the other. The master route's equivalences (V-full-group fp ⇒ quantum rigid; rigid ⇒ crossed product fp) check out elementary and sound, so the Z² crux is purely a representation-theoretic statement about one fp algebra — c9f9d0769
bh-free-61: For lattice-boundary hosts (𝒯_m, and hence closed MCG, Deligne, Lagrangian), Stein–Farley finiteness needs no tree for cofinality, since a Euclid potential makes edge splits cut every rational hyperplane. It fails at inheritance of descendance: an m=2 depth-5 descendant lies in a child without descending from it. So the one real combinatorial gate is a free-choice Serret theorem (synchronization), testable exactly by a row-reduction game — edge-splits-cut-every-rational-hyperplane, edge-split-descendants-are-not-factor-closed, unimodular-cells-synchronize-under-edge-splits (ef0882df7)
bh-free-55: Cantorized arithmetic actions × Cuntz (𝒯_m, 2V_Λ, W) are always simple and acyclic, via Li Cor D (cuntz-stabilized-minimal-full-groups-are-acyclic-and-simple), so only finite presentation matters. The pair-diagram/Stein–Farley engine needs finite dissection type, and that needs corners over finitely many number fields. Hyperplane walls (𝒯_m, MCG) and coset walls (W, GL_n(Z)) have rational corners. The Maslov walls of Λ_2 have corners over every Q(√2p) (maslov-cantorizations-have-no-finite-dissection-type). Deligne's lattice lifts only over flag varieties whose flags contain a Lagrangian (deligne-class-dies-over-flag-varieties-iff-flag-has-lagrangian). So at n = 2 no algebraic flag host carries both Deligne's lattice and diagrams. Deligne BH needs a non-algebraic "Lagrangian ?-function", a genuinely piecewise-linear Sp_2n(Z) action on 𝒯_m, or a diagram-free finiteness theorem.
- bh-self-similar [7ade83fcb] separated-automata-reduce-bh-to-fp-overgroups-of-the-base: when loops live on private letters, the automaton group sees its base only as an abstract group (germ transplant into an unrestricted wreath product), and its cycle germs can be saturated to (base ≀ finite) × finite. So BH via self-similar hosts drops one activity degree to a single input: a finitely presented AUTOMATON overgroup of the base (for linear activity: of a bounded group, e.g. Grigorchuk — bounded-automaton-groups-embed-in-fp-automaton-groups).
- [bh-free-20, 256f85294] germ-host-class-closed-under-sectioned-ascending-hnns: an INDUCTIVE BH host class 𝓒_d, defined by (C1) full, cone-closed, contains V_d; (C2) singularities in finitely many rational tail classes; (C3) F_inf stabilizers of finite rational sets in cone-supported subgroups. Seeds: all bounded V_dS. Hosts: every member is in an fp simple SV_W (type (A)). Closure: under ascending HNN K*_φ with φ acting on a cone as the identity copy (a right inverse of a virtual endomorphism) and F_inf tail group. Each step adds one singular tail class. So BH for such towers reduces to tail-group finiteness; the open program is more closure operations for 𝓒 and deciding which groups have cone-sectioned realizations.
- [bh-free-20, d090a714f] bartholdi-multi-substitution-hnn-surjects-onto-free-group: fp overgroups from L-presentations with k ≥ 2 substitutions surject onto F_k (Bartholdi thm:hnn's amenability clause needs k = 1). Amenable and BH hosts diverge here: extra stable letters are free for BH but fatal for amenability.
bh-free-59: DESIGN RULE for RF thickening hosts: take the acting group to be a higher-rank lattice. NST + CSP remove every free normal subgroup, hence every periodization fake; what is left is an arithmetic incidence question (can finitely many relations over SL_d(Z[1/2]) generate the divisibility data of P^{d-1}(Z/m)?), the same shape as presenting congruence quotients. — neumann-projective-host-candidate (71e81a4b0)
bh-free-13: Q-exact (full groups of amenable ample groupoids exact?) is not new: its first case is Thompson's F (F ≤ V = [[Cuntz groupoid]]; Scarparo: F exact ⟺ T exact, open). Exactness can refute BH only after F is proven exact; if F is non-exact, T and V are fp simple non-exact for free. On the proof side, a non-exact fp simple host is an ACTOR problem: any non-exact group with a type (A) action gives one (SV_G ⊇ G) — amenable-groupoid-full-groups-are-exact, some-nonexact-group-has-a-type-a-action
bh-free-53: Homotopy K-theory is never the obstruction for binary Cuntz–Pimsner hosts. Every binary self-similar completion O_ψ(K<X>) has KH = 0 (Lescure's 2026 KH sequence plus Gersten; the map 1 − d is −1 when d = 2). So G3r and A2 for bh-algebra's fp hosts are exactly nil-term (K_2-regularity) questions. The non-injective recursions that escape the complexity fence are exactly the ones where the left module has an annihilator and regularity fails — binary-self-similar-completions-over-free-bases-have-zero-kh
bh-free-02: Lift-presentation (fp of V_d(G)) is decided path by path (König): exponential activity and non-contraction are harmless when separators reset relators and letter types only decay; the one-configuration Wächter–Weiß counter is lift-presented by two commutators. The real obstruction is persistence along a RAY not fixed by all states (here the skip/continue lamplighter Z≀Z persistent along 0^ω), which loop retraction cannot see; a "ray retraction" theorem would be the missing classifying tool — lift-ideal-membership-is-decided-along-paths, single-configuration-check-mark-group-is-lift-presented
bh-synth-hosts (correction to my third line above): the dissection-category finiteness gate reaches only hosts with rational corners (𝒯_m for closed MCG, W for GL_n(Z)). Deligne via 2V_Λ(n) is out of reach of every diagram engine, since Maslov Cantorizations have no finite dissection type (bh-free-55, maslov-cantorizations-have-no-finite-dissection-type, f86da7972). Deligne needs a diagram-free finiteness theorem or a rational-corner recoordinatization.
- bh-g3-libridge: Li/Garside finiteness certificates compute normal forms, so with finite-state unit data they cap the WP at 2^{C l^2}; effective codings force RF (self-similar) units. So the rigid-SFT⇒Li bridge fails for every SFT with language harder than 2^{Cn^8}, whatever its rigidity. Hard hosts need non-finite-state RF units (the self-similar root) or a normal-form-free fp theorem (FTR) — li-coded-certificates-cannot-reach-hard-sft-hosts, finite-state-li-coded-full-groups-have-exponential-word-problem, finitely-coded-groupoid-unit-groups-are-residually-finite.
- bh-typeA-universal: Non-LCM finite presentation: a group acting on a simply connected union of finite poset intervals is fp given fp vertex stabilizers, f.g. COVER stabilizers (covers of any length) and finitely many orbits of covers and of intervals with disconnected open part — no lattice/joins/grading needed; infinitely many prime dissections only bite through simple connectivity of sublevels — interval-spherical-brown-criterion
bh-free-35: Complexity is conserved along HNN stages: an injective one-permutation stable letter over a type (A) actor is pinned by finitely many values, so fp + type (A) come free but hardness does not — the new orbit problem is the old one plus the orbit/subgroup distortion of orbit-finite edges. Identity edges must be bicommutant-closed (regular edges are; Mihailova fiber products never are). The only surviving hard shape is a regular orbit-finite edge with non-p.r. factorization distortion Γ = D·Γ_x — hnn-stages-add-no-complexity-beyond-edge-distortion (1141e0820)
bh-free-35: SV_G is conjugacy-rigid on its actor: at a constant point the germinal twist turns every conjugation between global twists into one in G, so ι(G) ∩ u^-1 ι(G) u is always a centralizer, SV_G repairs no missing conjugacy, and an HNN input with its base placed globally needs a base-faithful image of itself already in G. SV hosts help HNN-type inputs (BS, BG, mapping tori) only through brick-local twists — twisted-brin-thompson-global-twists-are-conjugacy-closed (1141e0820)
bh-free-35: Acylindrically hyperbolic inputs with trivial finite radical are highly transitive and MIF for free (Hull–Osin), so for them BH = PBH = "embed in an fp simple MIF group" with no permutational cost; for BG the ONLY failing type (A) clause is fg stabilizers — baumslag-gersten-group-is-highly-transitive-and-mif (6a8dbc894)
bh-free-51: Perfect inputs see only nonabelian point germs. Take any action by maps that are locally in a base with torsion-free abelian point germs (V, nV, Higman–Thompson, germ extensions of these, normal-form boundary groups), off finite sets. There, a f.g. subgroup J with H_1(J;Q)=0 is locally trivial at all but finitely many of its fixed points. Those points lie in the singular sets of J's generators and are permuted by C(J); in V or nV, Fix(J) is clopen. For closed Mod(S_g), g≥4, the subsurface groups are perfect, so any equivariant curve point p_d fixed by Stab^+(d) has Stab^+(d), including T_d, acting trivially near it. Moreover T_d ∈ ⟨Mod(S∖R) : R a one-holed torus ∋ d⟩, so twist data can never be localized to curve-indexed pieces. Rank-one or nV "exploded" hosts are dead. Hosts for perfect-rich inputs need nonabelian point germs (𝒯_m, GL(Z)-germs) or finitely many nonabelian singular germs. Cheap first test for any Cantor host: can the input's perfect subgroups be locally trivial at their fixed points? — perfect-subgroups-are-locally-trivial-in-abelian-germ-hosts (6963cfbdc)
bh-free-29 (G3g): P2′ splits cleanly. F(G_V × Λ⋉X) = <V(Λ), LC(X,V) ⋊ Λ>, and the two pieces meet in V × Λ. V(Λ) is fp for every X, so all rigidity sits in the Boolean-power crossed product N_X. N_X is the group twin of LC(X,k) ⋊ Λ, and its fp already forces quantum rigidity. The glue is a dynamics-free, self-similar amalgam; with an acyclic target, a central kernel suffices. The group side is harder than the ring side for one reason: groups lack the linear partition-of-unity relations, so "rigid ⇒ group fp" = ring rigidity + faithful linearization — v-times-sft-full-groups-split-into-two-wreath-pieces (and P2′a/P2′b nodes)
- bh-integrate (pass 4): The lanes keep re-proving B_A permanence one edge type at a time: four amalgam/HNN closure theorems, three copies of the coset-wreath lemma, and two Ã₂ PBH nodes 83 s apart. Consolidating them into one edge-realization closure theorem, plus `euclidean-building-lattices-lie-in-permutational-bh-class` for lattices, is the cheapest progress available (board/SYNTHESIS-DUPES.md items 1, 2, 7, 11).
bh-free-54: The synthesis top gate "Li without lcms" already exists: Thumann's operad-group theorem (arXiv:1409.1085, Thm 41762, read at source) gives F_∞ for any dissection-operad group from square filling + a FINITE SPINE of minimal common refinements. Several minimal refinements are allowed, so no LCM is needed. So for 𝒯_m, 2V_Λ and any operad-shaped extension-move category, fp = two checkable conditions (non-lcm-dissection-operad-groups-are-f-infinity).
- bh-g1-rigidsim: Copied or transported data are rigid for free: a copy rule is an exact operator identity in every quantum family. So over Λ_0 × Z^2 the skeleton and data layers always commute, whatever the growth of Λ_0; reading pressure is irrelevant to rigidity. — copy-constraints-make-simulation-skeleton-and-data-commute
- bh-g1-rigidsim: A simulation whose computation is determined by skeleton and data is rigid but only simulates SFTs. Useful simulations must carry undetermined (ambiguous) computation data, and G2 for simulations is exactly the unsplittable coupling of that ambiguity. — bounded-determination-simulations-only-realize-sft-shadows
- bh-self-similar [de682dfb7] rf-hosts-cannot-realize-the-lysenok-substitution: for a torsion group, any conjugator realizing a proper injective endomorphism generates the full ascending HNN extension; Grigorchuk's fp extension is not RF, so NO self-similar/automaton host can use the substitution that makes branch groups "fp up to an endomorphism" — the fp-automaton-overgroup input needs a new mechanism, while V-type hosts escape by being non-RF (prefix shift = t).
bh-refute: In piecewise-analytic 1-dim groups 'g ~ g^n' forces parabolic finite-order tangency at gap ends with a hyperbolic conjugator, so any finite conjugate-to-power cycle (Higman H4, Baumslag–Gersten) has trivial image; BH hosts for such inputs must be Cantor-type or non-analytic — analytic 1-dim dynamics cannot be universal — higman-group-embeds-in-no-lodha-moore-or-monod-group (verified)
bh-g2-obstruct: every classical or measure invariant (entropy, amenability/Følner, finite modifications, slabs) is FIRST ORDER (wall-type). Over Z^2 first order cannot kill Strategy 1 (Labbé is wall-rigid), and a G2 kill over amenable Λ would already answer Matui's question for expansive actions. So an obstruction must be genuinely contextual. — artifacts/gq-bh-bh-g2-obstruct-kill-attempt
bh-g2-obstruct: finite rigidity is necessary over EVERY group. A minimal SFT with one finitely supported modification is never quantum rigid, with no entropy needed, so no finite decoration of the crux object may be flippable. — minimal-sfts-with-a-finite-modification-are-not-quantum-rigid
bh-g2-obstruct: over Λ0 × H with H multi-ended (Λ0×Z, Λ0×F2, F2×F2), a rigid SFT is fixed by every thick slab except on one branch, i.e. a near-deterministic spacetime of H acting by automorphisms on a Λ0-fibre shift, which carries the aperiodicity. The F2 factor gives non-amenability but not rigidity; the design target is commutation propagating along expansive automorphisms (non-amenable Ledrappier). — quantum-rigid-sfts-over-products-have-a-single-free-branch
- bh-near-actions [5210b135f] algebraic-interval-exchange-groups-satisfy-boone-higman: a finiteness failure of a coding is often an orientation artifact, not an obstruction. Positive-base Pisot codings reach only fields with an isolated-sign unit (Q(√3) fails). Flipping orientation (y ↦ {−βy}) turns the needed finiteness into a classical SRS condition with a one-line alternating-sign witness set, and every real field has such units. So every f.g. algebraic IET group (flips allowed) is BH. For (★) and rigid-coding gates: when a finite-type coding fails finiteness, first vary the coding's sign/orientation twist before declaring the group side blocked. — negative-base-pisot-stein-groups-are-contracting-rsgs
- [bh-branch-in-v] Commensurating-action rigidity (MNZ product theorem) makes every tree-like faithful action of a branch-type group boundary-sandwiched, so a host cut out by a Schreier-graph class (V = context-free) can hold such an input only through the input's own boundary orbits: f.g. branch G embeds in V iff some subgroup of some G_xi has a context-free Schreier graph; with finite germs this needs an exponential-growth boundary orbit, so bounded-automaton branch groups need germ-dynamics hosts (Rover-Nekrashevych), never V. (branch-group-embeddings-in-v-are-boundary-sandwiched)
bh-synth-lead: Where the input sits decides the finiteness engine. G in the acting group needs FJ for the ring K-gate, so the group-side FTR (P2′, no normal form) is primary. A fixed FJ acting group with complexity in the SFT language needs an algebraic Kirchberg–Higman embedding U. Programmable Z² rigidity (G2-fp) serves both. — gq-bh-synthesis-master-route v3 (8d0279a62)
bh-one-relator: NEGATIVE — you cannot shrink a good identity edge P to P ∩ xPx^-1 by u ↦ w·xwx^-1. v acts as w on xPx^-1, so u g_1 u^-1 g_2 u g_1^-1 u^-1 collapses to g_1 g_2 g_1^-1 whenever g_2 ∈ P ∩ g_1^-1 P g_1 ∖ C. Pair stabilizers are not automatically good edges; the only open route over proper-power cyclic edges stays the decidable-wreath closure — recorded in baumslag-gersten-pbh-reduces-to-higman-group-and-a-lamp-wreath
- bh-invent-05: Centralizer realization (= identity-edge HNN and coset-lamplighter permanence of B_A) is functorial. It pulls back along homs to B_A groups, survives finite-index enlargement, and equals the trace of type (A) point stabilizers. So every normal N with G/N in B_A, and every C ⊇ [G,G], is realized. The residual crux is B_A-invisible decidable subgroups; canonically these are relator subgroups of decidable fp Q, where realization = the 'lamplighter shadow' Z/2≀_Q F_n ∈ B_A — centralizer-realizable-subgroups-are-pullback-closed (99a8ebf80)
bh-free-21: The Belk–Hyde–Matucci germ machine over V is Brin–Thompson-local: at rational points a normal germ group commutes with the period shift and is a spiral group C(C_2,Z)⋊V ≤ 2V, in which the shift is a product of two commutators (so charge obstructions do not apply, but no germ outside 2V is available). A universal compiler must take its base or germ groups from the input — rational-germ-groups-of-germ-extensions-of-v-embed-in-2v
bh-free-09: Quantum rigidity (G2) is closed under products and under deterministic finite-fibre extensions (constancy, copy, finite-group cocycle, bijective local transport along a monoid-generating set of directions). So uncoupled products create no faults, and non-rigidity can only come from genuinely free layers. In a simulation over Λ₀ × H the whole G2 crux sits in the hierarchical skeleton over H and the input layer; design rule 2 ("couple factors") is for existence (G1), not rigidity — quantum-rigidity-is-product-stable, deterministic-extensions-of-rigid-sfts-are-rigid
bh-g1-simulation: infinite f.g. simple (in general minimally almost periodic) inputs act trivially on the maximal equicontinuous factor of every minimal action. So a master-route SFT is never almost automorphic, and rigidity must be proved inside the MEF fibers, not through an odometer or torus parse. Functional plane couplings over almost-automorphic layers kill the input. — simple-subgroups-act-trivially-on-equicontinuous-factors (05f066167)
bh-g1-simulation: a non-amenable factor supplies grids for free (Barbieri–Sablik–Salo). G1 then reduces to (M2) plus minimal self-simulation (BSS Q:minimal). In every setting examined, the minimality gap is the same: diversify a relationally coupled field of hierarchy phases. — minimal-free-sft-via-minimal-self-simulation-of-squares, nonamenable-products-have-minimal-sft-extensions-of-free-inputs (05f066167)
bh-solvable: In a compact bundle with locally constant cocycles, a fg group's fiber cocycles see only one fg coefficient subgroup, so no divisible (Z[1/p], C_{p^∞}) center can sit in the fiber of a Cantor host. This is finite generation alone; unlike the finite-kernel case, no stabilizer condition enters. Hall/Abels-type non-RF inputs therefore need hosts with ENDS (unbounded cocycles, e.g. Q_p × circle) or a CONNECTED sheared circle coordinate (solenoid × R/Z) — compact-skew-products-cannot-carry-divisible-fiber-subgroups (8323a1cf9)
bh-solvable: Soluble BH = quasi-linear (free: metabelian, RF finite rank via Pyber–Segal, FP_∞ via Kropholler/MPN, all in B_A) + two known defects of linearity: (D) divisible torsion (non-RF) and (P) a commutator acting as a power map v ↦ v^m on a torsion-free abelian section (RF, not quasi-linear, e.g. the q-difference lamplighter). New soluble theorems need a host per defect, not better linearity — rf-soluble-minimax-groups-satisfy-permutational-bh, q-difference-lamplighter-is-not-quasi-linear
- bh-g3-steinfarley:
  - SFT Boolean inverse monoids are always fp (Stone + finite type), so every fp obstruction for SFT rings and V-full groups is transport of commutation between far supports. Quantum-family representations are exhausted exactly at rigidity, so P2′ needs a non-linear, combinatorial argument. — sft-groupoid-boolean-inverse-monoids-are-finitely-presented, quantum-family-representations-are-exhausted-at-rigidity (0cf7b14de)
  - The SFT splitting system has the cube (LCM) property. The P2′ obstruction is infinite type (amenable Λ, via an invariant measure), not missing lcms, so P2′ and the 𝒯_m dissection gate are different theorem types. Over non-amenable Λ, "translation-finitely coded" SFTs (a cylinder equal to a translate of a larger one) would restore finite type: compression supplied by Λ itself. — v-times-quantum-rigid-sft-full-groups-are-fp
bh-refute: Simplicity endgame: if every generator of G' but one odd-order c lies in N ⊴ G', then G'/N is cyclic, and an element of G' inverting c mod N forces c ∈ N — a reusable last step for simplicity of commutator subgroups of rearrangement/full-group hosts when no single rigid stabilizer contains the cycle — dendrite-rearrangement-group-g3-has-simple-commutator-subgroup (verified)
bh-g2-labbe: Determinism (any finite rules, both sides, any recoding) proves quantum rigidity only for SFTs without fault slabs in two directions; toral-rotation codings (Labbé) have four, so G2 for them needs a fault-crossing mechanism, and the open part sits on resonant separations across fault normals — determinism-closure-certifies-quantum-rigidity (264bcfe50)
bh-free-35: Automorphisms of a subshift see only its language (WP of any f.g. subgroup of Aut(Y) is p.r. relative to L(Y)). So in the master route's rule-8 time lifts, "complexity in Λ_0" IS "complexity in L(Y)": G2 must be a family Y_P of rigid minimal Z²-SFTs programmed by the input, never one universal tiling; Track A via time lifts needs Track B's programmable language. Complexity is carried, never created; the carrier is chosen after the input — time-lift-actors-carry-no-complexity-beyond-row-language (4f16b5b54)
- bh-major-mcg: a group's own commuting local moves give right-LCM codings for free. The two conditions are that moves with disjoint supports commute and that the wall deciding a move is unchanged by moves elsewhere; train-track splits satisfy both, while ambient unimodular dissections do not. The residue is always a Serret gate at non-generic points. — train-track-split-cones-have-least-common-descendants (b2da6e76d)
bh-free-12: G2 scale collapse — for an SFT with an injective constant-size block substitution σ(Ω) ⊆ Ω (e.g. DRS fixed-point tiles), a non-commuting D'-quantum tiling pulls back to a non-commuting N(D'−2)-tiling, so quantum rigidity is decided at one scale D_0 ≤ max(r,5); G2-fp's level induction must work at a fixed commutation radius ≤ 10, and one narrow wall kills a candidate at every scale — self-similar-sfts-are-quantum-rigid-iff-rigid-at-one-scale
- bh-major-gln: infinitely many primes are FREE inside fp decidable envelopes (one resolvent plus the Toeplitz shift inverts every N+k). They block only when forced into a centre (simple-quotient step) or into finite local data (Cantor hosts). Leavitt algebras split every integer with no mass, so (Q,+) ⊂ L^× as soon as one infinite-order unit commutes with a Leavitt copy. The prime question then becomes centralizer rank. Put arithmetic in the ACTING group. — leavitt-unit-commuting-with-a-leavitt-copy-gives-q-in-units, gq-bh-major-gln-skeleton (3714a2f15)
bh-free-37: Recurrence is anti-local. Over the uniform product measure, the local lemma cannot certify recurrence at infinitely many scales next to any positive-density constraint: the success rate per site is δ_r → 0, while the cost per site is fixed. So minimal (free) subshifts need hierarchical entropy reduction, i.e. finite libraries in a deterministic computable skeleton. On groups with no finite quotients that skeleton is non-profinite, which is the same kind of object as the G2 rigid codings — local-lemma-cannot-certify-multiscale-recurrence (b81707e5f)
bh-refute: Generation rank is never an obstruction in dynamical hosts with a finite-order shift over a Sidon family of disjoint pieces (Bleak–Elliott–Hyde packing): any finite local data packs into one element and returns as commutators — an in-host HNN/Schupp principle — lodha-simple-group-s-is-two-generated (verified)
bh-free-58: Arithmetic rigidity at infinity: a piecewise-affine (rcwa) conjugator between multiplicative S-unit dynamics must be linear on the far ends of every infinite orbit (finite-piece counting). So the non-S cofactor is an end invariant, and one additive twist is detected. This yields Sigma^0_1-complete conjugacy in fp simple CT_P(Z), and is a reusable non-conjugacy / non-embedding test for RCWA-type hosts — ct-z-conjugacy-problem-is-undecidable
bh-major-branch-b: branch hosts relocate, not escape, the depth-transport problem. Over V the growing alphabets and Alt scaffold are free (V holds all finite groups; scaffold commutes with zoom), and fp needs a finite nonabelian recurrence pinning the input's residual-chain actions; one-step recurrences = virtual endomorphisms. Regular-tree hosts of the branch group are impossible (it contains Alt(m) for unbounded m). — branch-host-v-recodings-need-recurrent-residual-chains, bishop-schesler-branch-hosts-act-on-no-regular-rooted-tree
- bh-g2-f2f2: For a coset shift (right-Δ-invariant configurations), quantum rigidity is commutation carried across the pair types Δ\Λ/Δ. Short types commute for free, and determinism supplies descent between the rest. A finite double-coset space makes every coset shift rigid, but (IS) homogeneity puts constants in every orbit closure. A large stabilizer rotates every determinism rule into every frame, so cyclic descent over F₂×F₂/diagonal collapses to finite subshifts. A rigid free minimal SFT needs stabilizers that merge pair types but fix a direction, so that determinism runs one-sided as in Ledrappier's rows. — coset-invariant-sft-rigidity-descends-along-double-cosets, two-sided-free-group-coset-sfts-collapse-under-cyclic-descent (e9de95fc2)
- bh-major-product: every host machine on main squares itself: the Cuntz cone shift ([[G x R_2]] ⊇ its square), the Leavitt corner map (R x R ↪ R, so units, GL_n and E_n square themselves; St_n loses exactly one K_2) and the Brin–Thompson clopen stabilizer. So product closure (SQ) is never a separate gate for a constructive BH route; it comes with the route. A counterexample to (SQ) must embed in no self-square fp simple group. — cuntz-stabilized-full-groups-contain-their-own-squares, leavitt-pair-unit-hosts-contain-their-own-squares (b043f3be1)
- bh-major-product: with Titz Mite–Witzel in B_A (Euclidean-building theorem), every fp simple group recorded on main except W, W_(P,ℓ) and the Leavitt tensor units is in B_A. JE is open only for pairs touching those three, and the Leavitt one reduces to three gates for (L⊗L)^× ∈ B_A. — fp-simple-groups-have-the-joint-embedding-property Attempt 5
bh-free-59: SCHUR MULTIPLIER OBSTRUCTION (supersedes my fake-action lessons whenever the acting group is FP_3). If Γ is FP_2, N ◁ Γ is perfect, and H_3(Γ/N) is fg, then H_2(N)_Γ is fg (LHS: d_2 source vanishes, d_3 source is H_3(Q)). A thickening host has infinitely many retracting normal coordinate factors A_m, each carrying a surviving Z/2, so it is never fp, whatever the rigidity of Q. Corollary: an FP_2 group with a normal ⊕A_m has a quotient with non-fg H_3. Check H_2 of the fibre before designing any host N ⋊ Q. — schur-multiplier-obstruction-kills-thickening-hosts (aa0ae6e48)
bh-g2-permutive: permutive rigidity comes from SEEDLESS determinism, so every fault is one-sided and total; a free permutive SFT must be semi-expansive in three positively spanning directions with rows of complexity >= (m+1)^2/8 moved non-shift-like by a bipermutive CA. No known aperiodic construction has three semi-expansive directions; if none exists, rigid free SFTs need contextual (two-sided-fault) rigidity. — triangle-permutive-sfts-have-one-sided-total-faults (ec8c1c33b)
bh-refute: A self-embedding that kills H^2 (for F: δ(f)=f*(RfR), the flip reverses Brown's product and acts by −1 on H_2) makes every central extension split on a copy; transferable test: central extensions of a host are harmless when some self-embedding is zero on H^2 — Deligne-type bases (Sp_2g(Z)) are exactly those lacking one — central-extensions-of-thompson-f-contain-f (verified)
bh-invent-09: Gate U has an exact interface. A fixed-group Leavitt/full-group host sees the input only through bounded-displacement actions on the scaffold Λ. Move tables of such an action form an effectively closed Λ-subshift carrying WP(Γ) in its language, and K-theory is paid once by Farrell–Jones for Λ. On free orbits the input's growth and separation profile must fit inside Λ: no hyperbolic or low-sep scaffold is universal, and monsters need non-free orbits or an fp FJ group with expanders. The target is embedding decidable groups in the uniformly bounded wobbling group of Λ×N. — translation-like-inputs-embed-in-full-groups-over-a-fixed-group, translation-like-hosts-are-capped-by-separation-profiles (2fa8570ac)
- [bh-invent-06, 59cea071f] telescopic-germ-bases-in-v-germ-extensions-are-v-separated: a telescope is a group acting on every annulus around a point through one endomorphism's orbit, and it is the canonical cross-depth rule. Its germ group is (P/K_inf)*sigma, whose base is the direct limit, so one endomorphism gives only Z[1/m]-type divisibility. Inside ANY finite germ extension of V, faithful telescopic germ bases are EXACTLY the dynamically V-separated groups (the converse of bh-free-22). A host that simulates itself at every scale dissolves into V: sigma^n(h) is in V eventually, and simple, FW or Kazhdan simulated subgroups embed in V. Telescopes are a finiteness engine, not an embedding engine: they never carry a simple input upward that the seed lacks.
- [bh-invent-06, 59cea071f] telescope-fixed-points-are-singular-on-a-parse-shift: the Kleene fixed point theta = (s, theta, theta, ...) exists uniquely. It is a two-state transducer, fixes the PARSE SHIFT of never-terminating hierarchies (a golden-mean SFT), is singular on all of it, and carries the seed group in its germ at every point of it. So genuine self-simulation turns finite singular sets into SFT singular sets. The finiteness theorem it needs is P2′ over the parse SFT, not BHM. Along one chain this is PSPACE-capped; over a one-ended Lambda with locally unique parse it is the group-side twin of G2-fp (OPEN: self-simulating-hosts-over-one-ended-parse-shifts).
bh-free-60: Infinite vertex groups of a free product become points of the compactified Bass–Serre boundary and carry their whole homology (H_n(D ⋉ X) = H_n(B) for n ≥ 1, H_0 = Z via a signed Euler measure); finite vertex groups contribute nothing. So Z/2 * B is an isotropy host for ANY B with simple D(F) — and fp of such hosts cannot be read from isotropy/homology (unsolvable-WP perfect B ⇒ non-fp D(F)); infinite-emitter hosts need a WP-sensitive fp argument — free-product-boundary-groupoids-carry-the-vertex-group-homology (b5ce5bff2)
bh-algebra: A fixed amenable acting group of subexponential growth (Z²) buys K-theory but loses all permutational room for Kazhdan inputs: [[P⋉X]] has no infinite Kazhdan subgroup (amenable orbits kill infinite orbits, and expansion bounds the finite ones). So on Track B the canonical inputs H_Γ must enter through the Leavitt tree or through EL_3 over an embedded ring. Complexity from the language, rigidity from matrices — kazhdan-groups-avoid-full-groups-of-subexponential-actions
- bh-groupoid [05fca9eb0] degree-coboundary-subgroups-of-coded-full-groups-are-rf: in a finitely coded host, an input whose level (degree) cocycle is a continuous coboundary is virtually a subgroup of a finite product of unit groups, hence RF. In the minimal case, bounded displacement on one orbit suffices. So every non-RF input (simple, quasisimple) must scale unboundedly along every orbit, as lattices do on boundaries, and so must every Kazhdan input when the units are a-T-menable. A Li proof of P2′ over St_10(R_L) needs scaling, not SFT rigidity.
bh-refute: Integral primitive representatives with multiplicative reduction at good primes force the linear part of any f.g. group of birational maps over Q to be S-arithmetic (hence RF), so algebraic host families can never contain divisible linear subgroups like (Q,+); a f.g. host for GL_n(Q) must realize Q non-linearly or non-RF — fg-cremona-subgroups-have-arithmetic-linear-part (verified)
- bh-major-branch-a: Bishop–Schesler's growing alphabets recode (c_n(x_n)=0) into an annular host over V, so the branch route IS the one-point germ route. Every fp annular host has an HNN-recursive germ group ⟨L, s | s^-1 L_1 s = θ(L_1)⟩ (Bieri–Strebel). Storing a chain one finite quotient per depth gives H≀Z (disjoint codes) or free zoom, both non-fp. The only live one-point shape is a non-ascending θ: an fp ascending driver pinning passenger data — fp-annular-germ-groups-are-hnn-recursive, depth-disjoint-spinal-hosts-have-wreath-germ-groups.
- bh-major-cube-a: Li's (t<d) is loop-richness. Label-injective (special/RAAG) cube codings are deterministic: one self-return per layer at every power. Additive gradings, which make "power" work, exist on cube categories only for products of trees. Non-product flats therefore need an overcoding into a loop-rich shift (Salo-style) or a finiteness theorem for deterministic codings (primitive type matrix instead of diagonal ≥ 2). — commutative-cube-degree-maps-force-tree-products, raag-cube-codings-fail-li-multiplicity-at-every-power (29029fde3)
bh-verify-euclid: the general-type Euclidean-building B_A theorem checks out (PASS). Beyond Ã₂, minimal common extensions of boxes are not unique, and the node's disjoint-ideal argument (not link girth) is the right fix. The remaining risk for the Titz Mite–Witzel consequence is the unrefereed Ciobotaru–Le Bars topological freeness — gq-bh-verify-euclid-report (84896de5e)
bh-refute: 'Simple' (Thompson/Belk–Zaremsky) and 'finitely presented' (Clapham) come separately and free for every decidable input; the standard simple envelopes use the translation action, which never has finitely many orbits on pairs — so any BH proof must replace the regular action by a type-(A) (or finiteness-certified) action; the regular action is exactly the wrong one — gl-n-q-embeds-in-fg-simple-and-fp-decidable-groups (verified)
- bh-invent-04: Busemann transplant. Chamber stabilizers of torsion-free building lattices embed in Z^r via Busemann translation. So any Z^r-SFT that is deterministic from an INTERIOR cone direction, e.g. Kari–Papasoglu corner-deterministic tiles through an open-cone basis, lifts coherently across the branching to a FREE SFT over the lattice (given X_Ω SFT). Rank-2 Kazhdan, one-ended, non-amenable lattices thus inherit Z²'s aperiodicity and computation; rigidity transfer reduces to centrality of germ idempotents — busemann-transplants-give-free-sfts-on-building-lattices (5cb681f07)
bh-openq-papers: Gate M2 (effective minimal free subshifts) splits cleanly. Freeness + a computable point + computable central recurrence are FREE for every decidable f.g. group (Álvarez López 1807.09256's hierarchical colouring; its top-down limit is coherent at the base point, so no compactness is needed). The only wall is central→global recurrence (seams between adjacent clusters), and it is real: effective subshifts on decidable groups realize every Π⁰₁ Medvedev degree (Barbieri–Carrasco-Vargas 2406.12777). Minimal free SFTs already exist over one-ended non-amenable F_n×Z (ABHT 2204.11492) — decidable-fp-groups-have-effective-minimal-free-subshifts (Attempt 7), free-times-z-carries-a-minimal-free-sft
bh-houghton-presentation: For "is some f.g. subgroup ascending under a fixed automorphism?" questions, first reformulate as Σ^1-sidedness (Brown), then look for an end- or orbit-counting symmetry. The shift at infinity acts on RCWA germs by drifting offsets, and a calibrated search found no one-sided closure. The likely truth is the symmetric statement, "f.g. shift-similar ⇒ strongly shift-similar". — fg-infinite-shift-similar-groups-are-strongly-shift-similar
- bh-g2-fixedpoint-b: Over non-amenable groups quantum rigidity comes cheap from COMPRESSION. The end shift of F_n has edge projections that force monotone commuting chains along every geodesic, so it is rigid at every scale over every field, with no determinism and no hierarchy. Its product over F_m×F_n is a rigid, minimal, topologically free SFT over a one-ended non-amenable group. The G2 gap is therefore FREENESS: every hyperbolic element fixes ends. Free decorations built from Busemann/Z^r fibres re-import an amenable fibre, where the Z² invariant-measure heuristic applies again. — free-group-boundary-shifts-are-quantum-rigid (201fb24d4)
- bh-invent-01: In full groups of G_V × H, cone-localized global bisections τ_u(φ) slide a condition's window anywhere while commuting with everything on other cones. So every relation between elements with DISJOINT V-supports follows from bounded-diameter relators (diameter ≤ 1 for site conditions), and the finite-presentation problem (P2′, G3g) lives entirely in the fibre: commutation of conditioned copies of one V-element at distance, exactly what quantum rigidity measures. — v-cone-sliding-localizes-disjoint-support-relations (3f547b04c)
- bh-invent-08: Renormalization in nV is a compiler. One identity "phi = brick diagonal delta_e on generators" gives G*_phi in (k+1)V for the price of one fresh coordinate, with no finiteness proof needed (brick-diagonal-ascending-hnns-embed-in-brin-thompson-groups). Products and block rotations of SMART odometers realize every odd block-companion dilation (monomial-renormalization-compiler-gives-dilation-groups-in-nv).
- bh-invent-08: Drift is the compiler's conserved quantity. delta_e(b) = b^m forces zero drift, while every compiled stable letter has signed drift in its fresh coordinate, so compiler steps cannot be stacked into BS chains, H4 or BG (compiled-bs-stable-letters-are-never-rediagonalized). Hierarchies need a drift-free power-conjugator inside the old coordinates: a machine whose renormalization map is itself renormalizable.
bh-one-relator: A doubling swindle inside a self-similar presentation makes its kernel RELATIVELY PERFECT (K = [K, Γ~]): the V-conjugate copies give x = x·x modulo [K, Γ~]. So "central kernel + acyclic target ⇒ retract" can only ever prove the presentation EXACT (K = 1), never split off a nonzero kernel. bh-refute: Permanence one singular orbit at a time: in a cone-closed full host with rational singularities and an F_∞ stabilizer package, a cone-sectioned ascending HNN adds exactly one new singular tail class whose germ group is an ascending HNN of the tail group, and BHM's SingFix induction (base = old package) propagates F_∞ — the template for closure theorems of BH host classes — germ-host-class-closed-under-sectioned-ascending-hnns (verified)

L carries non-soficity but no complexity. Gate U therefore needs language-conditioned Leavitt isometries, the two interleaved. - bh-g1-effective: (M2) is automatic with finite quotients: Toeplitz subshifts from normal chains are FREE, so M2 holds for all effectively RF groups (all fp RF, all fg linear). bh-synth-lead: Carrier principle. The input's complexity AND coarse geometry must be carried by the acting group Λ. - bh-invent-07: Thumann's operad finiteness engine needs only F_∞ transformations, not F_∞⁺: pull-through subgroups are finite-orbit stabilizers. But finitely generated operads make transformations act on finite class sets, so a no-finite-quotient input (e.g. simple Kazhdan) placed in the transformations lands in a label kernel and never reaches the dynamics. Programmable hosts need infinitely many elementary moves permuted oligomorphically: the operadic actor problem, a strict generalization of type (A). — thumann-operad-groups-need-only-f-infinity-transformations, finite-type-operad-groups-split-over-a-label-kernel, oligomorphic-operads-have-f-infinity-groups (b50f07270)
bh-free-61: In rank two, descent is governed by shared rays. The link of a ray has rank one, where Stern–Brocot is complete, so nested unimodular cells sharing a ray always descend. Hence synchronization for 𝒯_2 holds at every rational or rationally constrained direction, and in rank m rational directions reduce to Sync_{m−1}. So the whole Stein–Farley gate of the lattice-boundary hosts is a Diophantine shared-vertex problem at totally irrational directions (search: all 10310 stuck 3×3 matrices up to entry sum 36 won) — rank-two-synchronization-off-totally-irrational-rays (1a5d65604)
- bh-invent-03: In the Boolean-inverse-monoid category, finite presentation of Λ⋉X (with or without V) is exactly finite type, because idempotents commute by axiom. So the plain "groupoid Higman embedding" is just gate P1. All of BH's difficulty is the units functor B ↦ F(𝒢), which loses "idempotents commute" and must be paid for by rigidity, i.e. a finitely presented Steinberg algebra (transfer conjecture). - bh-g2-fixedpoint-b: FOLDS IMPORT THE FIBRE'S RIGIDITY PROBLEM UNCHANGED. Path-folding (ABHT on F_n×Z, unimodular GBS, BS(2,3)) and Busemann transplants realise every fibre configuration along a 1-Lipschitz map. So a noncommuting fibre family pulls back at the same scale: the total shift is rigid ⇒ the fibre is rigid. Non-amenable tree directions supply a rigid skeleton and freeness, but not fibre rigidity. bh-invent-11: Two new live BH separators, both about subgroups of fp simple groups (the fp simple groups themselves satisfy them trivially). bh-invent-11: (2) P_eff: residually finite subgroups must be effectively residually finite. It fails at Rauzy's L(A). So a universal host must hide finite quotients at a non-recursive depth. bh-invent-10: The kernel is never the type (A) obstruction. Aut_S(S*F_n) acts FAITHFULLY on the orbit of (t,1,…,1) in Hom_S(S*F_n, S*<t>) for every S ≠ 1 (substitution + MIF of free products). - bh-invent-12 [9b3fdd027] a2-lattice-boundary-skew-shifts-are-quantum-rigid: FINITE PRESENTATION IS A RIGIDITY CERTIFICATE. The necessity theorem needs only topological freeness (one free point per cylinder; topologically-free-subshift-full-groups-force-quantum-rigidity). So any fp V-stabilized full group of a lattice's boundary action certifies quantum rigidity of the boundary subshift. Rigidity is transitivity of "≤" between idempotents, with commutation assumed only at distance ≤ 2. This is the rank-one prototype of "hyperbolic geometry supplies quantum rigidity".
bh-major-branch-c: pinning is definability, so pinned passengers are fixed by the driver's Aut(V)-symmetries; natural drivers (conjugation, reversible machine as conjugation, cone copy, diagonal doubling) are self-similar in Aut(V) and pin only bounded or ascending passengers; a non-ascending passenger needs a transport definable over the driver but unrealized in the base — pinned-annular-passengers-are-class-functions-of-the-driver, pinned-passengers-collapse-when-the-shifted-system-is-solvable (ba298cb26)
bh-free-54: For a PERFECT group inside a purely infinite minimal full group, a Thompson-like host needs no coding of the group: an Ore dissection operad whose group contains one germ-generating subgroup (e.g. GL_n(Z), where transvections are single carets) contains all swaps, hence D([[𝒢]]) (Matui), hence the perfect group. Type (A) holds without fullness. So closed MCG BH = Ore + finite spine for the edge-split operad in rank 6g−6 (ore-operad-groups-contain-the-derived-full-group, closed-mcg-bh-via-edge-split-operad).
bh-invent-perron: Li F_n finiteness needs only a strictly-increasing positive weight on cylinder types with finite sublevels; his (t<d) (diagonal >=2) is the special case w=1. Replacing it by "type-substitution matrix primitive with Perron eigenvalue >1" (Perron left eigenvector = the weight) gives a finiteness theorem covering deterministic label-injective codings: free-group boundary full groups are F_infty un-recoded (diagonal 1, lambda=2k-1). Loop-richness is a Perron property, not a diagonal one. Nodes: perron-substitution-finiteness-for-garside-full-groups (4e48d1730).
- bh-invent-06b: self-simulation along one chain with a FINITE seed group is exactly the contracting-RSG regime (finite nucleus {id, θ_h, φ_h, h|_β}); BBMZ's thrm:fin_pres absorbs an uncountable SFT singular set for free, while infinite seeds leave every contracting host. - bh-gate-u-wobble: for simple inputs, gate U over a fixed scaffold Λ is exactly a coarsely dense injective Lipschitz realization of some Schreier quotient K\Cay(G) in Λ (effectively closed from WP(G) alone); for Kazhdan inputs these are one-ended and uniformly nonamenable, killing amenable (so Track B over Z² via full groups), polynomial-growth and virtually free scaffolds; F₂×F₂ passes every test and is the sharp open case. — simple-inputs-in-wobbling-hosts-are-dense-schreier-realizations, kazhdan-inputs-need-one-ended-nonamenable-wobbling-scaffolds (52bfd4df9)

- bh-g3-topfree [2914b739e] v-times-tree-boundary-full-groups-are-f-infinity + v-times-rigid-topologically-free-sft-full-groups-are-fp: COMPRESSION CODINGS ARE ONE-SIDED SFT GROUPOIDS. - bh-g2-abh [3c8b41742] path-folds-of-rigid-expansive-z2-sfts-are-quantum-rigid (now ESTABLISHED, lane proof): TREE FOLDS ARE RIGIDITY-NEUTRAL. The fold's flow skeleton is automatically CENTRAL. The reason is that commutation, unlike operators, propagates one tree step at a time along the backward-arrow chain Q_1 ⊑ Q_k, and the flow is constant on t-cosets, so each step needs only a radius-R window of a row that already commutes with Q_1. No far letter is ever expanded, which is what stalled (C). With central flow, letters at equal horoheights coincide, and a section family is a quantum family for the fibre. So ABHT's F_n×Z SFT is rigid ⟺ its Z² fibre X_0 is rigid. General mechanism: a skeleton idempotent that is invariant along the decoration's deterministic direction, and dominated by a chain under which the decoration is transported, is central. Master-route consequence: E2 along folds over F_n×Z (and H×F_n) IS the amenable fibre gate. Non-amenable folding adds no rigidity; win E2 in the fibre (self-similar certificates) or by a non-fold mechanism. Try chain transport on the Busemann (C).
- bh-gln-centralizer [a892110822] monomial-leavitt-units-are-never-self-doubling: isotropy-twisted induced (Chen) modules turn every hyperbolic periodic orbit of a full-group element into a finite-dimensional spectral block that doubling cannot absorb (lag-0 cycles are blind to the twist). So absorption (t ≅ t⊕t, the engine of infinite divisibility) can never be fed by full-group/monomial units over any field; divisible inputs (Q in GL_n(Q)) must ride on the ACTING GROUP of the host (carrier principle), and R2 is parked at a non-monomial unital L⊗F[u^±1] ↪ L.
bh-free-35 [9c360c2ca]: For fixed-point (self-simulating) tile sets E2 is a ONE-SCALE problem in both directions. Decimation sends local families to local families of the same tile set at the same scale (phases are central), so commutation up to range N(2D+2)-2 climbs every level: rigid iff that bounded-range commutation holds (semi-decidable, finite certificate) — fixed-point-tile-set-rigidity-is-a-bounded-range-test. Dually, substituting two configurations that agree on ONE column and differ on both sides refutes rigidity at every scale, so rigid self-similar SFTs need one-sided determining columns/rows and no information-free macrotile column (Ledrappier, self-similar with N=2, calibrates) — self-similar-sft-thin-walls-refute-quantum-rigidity.
- bh-major-cube-b [ba9176b48a] perron-weights-do-not-give-li-finiteness: HEIGHTS ARE CHEAP; FINITENESS IS MERGE PACKING. Li's F_n proof needs (a) a height (a piece count or Perron weight works) and (b) (Pack): objects far from the base contain many disjoint mergeable generator patterns. (t<d) is a local guarantee of (b). The F_k reduced-word coding has a Perron weight yet infinitely many non-simply-connected Witzel complexes x_N = (a, a^-1, N b, N b^-1), so the Perron substitution proof is invalid (conclusion true for graphs only via Matui recoding). Packing fails exactly along THIN CYCLES: a hitting set R of types plus a cycle with one R-child per step. Test every deterministic host coding for thin cycles first; the open gate for Q1.15 is a thin-cycle-free recoding of C_X that keeps lcms, not a better eigenvalue.
- [bh-free-16, 541875e2cd] t-check-contains-no-q-rtimes-2-z: blow-ups do not evade boundary arithmetic. An expanding almost-automorphism pulls the boundary values of positive-mass cells back to the 2-power preimage of ONE finite set; differences of such sets hold only bounded odd denominators (irrational boundary points included). Mass-zero cells (Denjoy gaps) are never read, so the obstruction survives every monotone semiconjugacy. Result: A x|_2 Z lies in T-check only for A = Z[1/2]; every single-radix real/circle coordinate is dead for Q x| <d>, exact or semiconjugate. Aff(Q)/GL_n(Q) arithmetic must live in multi-radix (Stein) cells, dilation-mixed coordinates, or the acting group.
- bh-g3-tfc [00a44f5d54] free-sft-hosts-admit-no-finite-type-coding: FINITELY CODED COMPRESSION IS CONTRACTION, AND CONTRACTION HAS FIXED POINTS. Any finite graph-directed (Garside) coding of an ample groupoid gives a contracting loop; in G_V × (Λ⋉X) its germ has a Λ-label λ ≠ 1 with an attracting fixed point (never central). So a free X admits no Li/Stein–Farley/Witzel/SWZ coding, for any Λ, rigid or not, and free-form P2′ is an infinite-type theorem ((A_N) on the non-cocompact filtration). Flexible finite type with translation cone types also kills every infinite invariant-measure factor. Master route: keep freeness and invent an infinite-type Brown criterion, or switch E3 → E3′ (topologically free) and inherit Li.
- bh-invent-12 [377ea1e4d] attracting-boundary-factors-fix-commuting-actions: ATTRACTORS FIX EVERYTHING THAT COMMUTES WITH THEM. One element with a dense-basin attracting point makes factor maps onto a minimal system unique. So in Λ₁ × Λ₂ the input factor fixes every boundary-type factor of Λ₂ pointwise, and time lifts over every compression-rigid row built so far (F_d boundary, boundary products, Ã₂ skew shifts) carry only finite groups. The input acts only inside fibres over the boundary, whose stabilizers are ≤ Z^r for building lattices. Compression rigidity is proximal: it rigidifies only what the input cannot touch. Geometric scaffolds hand the input's problem back as Λ₁ × Z^r, i.e. the Z² gate. The only exit is type (A) on the input's own points, and Thompson labels (LC(C,K)⋊V) cannot manufacture it.

Linear hosts use commensurated SUBSPACES (t^n F_2[t]^3 for SL_3(F_2[t])), which nothing collapses. So over Z and over F_2, GL_3 of the crossed product contains infinite Kazhdan groups, although the full group has none. A paradoxical decomposition is an SFT, so the Leavitt algebra (and non-soficity) is internal over a scaffold EXACTLY when it is non-amenable. Pose gate U linearly over one fixed non-amenable FJ scaffold, with the oracle indexed by the whole scaffold. Track B then has Track A's shape and shares its gates.
- bh-ref-kourovka-b [fac0259d6b] ct-z-finite-cycle-problems-are-undecidable + ct-p1-and-ct-p2-always-generate-ct-p1-union-p2, referee PASS: THE SHARED 2-COLOUR IS THE ENGINE. Every CT_P(Z) contains all 2-power class maps, and that one colour both routes (spare mod-4 boxes, which give the 21.75 join theorem) and reversibilizes (doubling f to f and f^{-1} on 2X gives a product of two involutions, which is 21.74). Any host whose colours share a common Cantor factor inherits both.
- bh-invent-10 [7ac816a5c] free-rewritable-tape-layers-kill-finite-presentation: NO FREE LAYER. A layer with no local constraint admits slot families: properly colour the 2D-neighbourhood graph, one tensor slot per colour, and put far same-colour sites in rotated bases. These commute on every ball but not globally, and rewriting power (Turing tape, homoclinic surgery) only upgrades idempotents to matrix units. So minimality bought from noise, a scratch tape or surgery always costs finite presentation, and E1 cannot be bypassed. The only freedom an fp host can afford is non-local (hierarchy phases); every symbol must be pinned by constraints, and that is what E2 pays for. Byproduct: 3V_□ (the area-preserving part of 3V) and the Turing–Thompson groups over any Λ are simple, finitely generated (sketch), and not fp.
- [bh-free-16, 1d38dc7fda] one-coordinate-registers-admit-no-dilation: equivariance forces locality, locality forces arithmetic. A dilation normalizing a register copy of Q has twist f = pi∘t − d·pi invariant under the copy; small register rotations make f locally constant and force t to read the register only through its own coordinate, then positive-shadow box chains put all denominators in one f.g. subgroup of R (rational part cyclic). So in nV / SV_G / 2V_tau NO element, however it mixes other coordinates, dilates a one-coordinate register of Q. Divisible inputs need continuously varying twists (odometer germs across coordinates) or registers spread over several coordinates.
- bh-major-onerel [398b7ad029] three chain-host nodes: A CHAIN CONFIGURATION IS SPECTRAL, AND THEOREM 2.1 NEEDS ASCENDING GERM TOWERS. (i) b dilates a^n c a^-n by 2^(2^n): unboundedly many eigenvalues, so Noetherian abelian sections (metabelian, linear, poly-ascending of height ≤3) never hold one. (ii) Germ groups that grow by ascending HNN steps over the zoom make every SingFix group an ascending HNN tower over Fix_V, hence F_∞: a checkable regime beyond Cor. 2.10 (doubling extension of V is an instance). (iii) PL square maps bend on every dyadic scale, so their sparse commuting defects kill [c_1, σ^k c_1 σ^-k]: PL germ realizations of G_1 are never faithful. Target for G_1/H4/BG hosts: non-ascending germ towers with fp SingFix groups, or twisted induced-lamp modules.
- bh-pal-steinberg [a7d68b30b]: the Leavitt swindle is kernel-checked in Lean with no field hypothesis. It needs only an injective ring map F → S into a ring with a Leavitt pair commuting with its image, plus a perfect St_V(F), and it embeds E_V(F) in St_{3|V|+1}(S). Morita collapse removes rank, and fp S gives fp St_n(S), n ≥ 5. So the embedding half of every Leavitt ring host is closed; what BH still owes on the ring side is only the K-gate (a simple quotient that keeps the input).
- bh-typeA-universal: Every move language is a full group in disguise. The boundary image of ANY symmetric operad group (no finite type, arbitrary transformations) is a full Cantor group, clopen transitive iff the color monoid has one nonzero class. So an fp boundary-faithful operad host is its own type (A) actor, a simple one is automatically faithful, and operadic/Thompson-like/groupoid hosts certify exactly B_A and never separate BH from PBH. With bounded common refinements, orbit finiteness even makes the transformation group an actor on its moves: operads beat SV_G only through unbounded refinement depth (the non-LCM regime), where short pair relations must generate long ones. — fp-symmetric-operad-groups-lie-in-the-permutational-class, bounded-refinement-operads-reduce-to-their-carriers (4060cc1fc)
- bh-major-product [2d9a15b01] projective-leavitt-tensor-units-lie-in-the-permutational-class + twisted-integral-affine-groups-lie-in-the-permutational-class: PBH = fp OF THE HOST on both machines. Central scalars cost one tensor doubling (u ↦ u ⊗ (u*)^(-1), Leavitt PGL ↪ GL); a finite K_0 class on clopens costs nothing (act on ordered partitions into unit-class pieces, the V_(ℓ,r) trick). So every recorded fp simple group is in B_A, and a BH/PBH separator (Q3.4) or a JE/SQ counterexample must be an fp simple group built by neither full groups nor Leavitt-tensor units.
- bh-p2b-exact [7054f9da2] gluing-kernels-descend-from-the-full-shift: PRESENTATION KERNELS DESCEND; THE FULL SHIFT IS THE UNIVERSAL CASE. For [[G_V x (Lambda ⋉ X)]] with X topologically free, the gluing kernel K_X is the restriction image of the ambient kernel K_Y, and F(T_X) = F(T_Y) modulo one conditioned V-element per forbidden pattern. Over infinite Lambda the full shift is itself topologically free, so P2′b for every topologically free subshift over A is ONE statement about A^Lambda: FSG(Lambda, A). P2′b(X) is exactly finite presentation of F(T_X) relative to the Boolean power N_X, so it is NECESSARY for fp. Rigidity (P2′a) lives on X, and exactness (P2′b) lives on the canonical full shift, so the choice of SFT in (★′) never affects P2′b. Proofs descend and refutations climb. Osajda exactness (property A) is unrelated: it is not presentation exactness.
- bh-major-onerel [f0eb931cdd] finite-hirsch-length-soluble-groups-hold-no-bs-chain: RANK IS A SPECTRUM BUDGET. In a soluble group of Hirsch length h an element has at most h infinite-order eigen-elements with distinct integer dilation rates (one eigenvector count on the derived series). The BS chain needs rates 2^(2^n) for all n, so polycyclic, minimax, and every ascending HNN tower (so every cheap BHM zoom-tower germ host) is excluded. Hosts for G_1/H4/BG must have infinite rank: non-ascending SingFix finiteness, or fp soluble groups of infinite Hirsch length (Baumslag-type).
- bh-major-mcg-2 [abd02ad86] edge-split-sync-reduces-to-totally-irrational-shared-rays: SYNCHRONIZATION IS AN INDUCTION ON RANK WITH ONE NEW INPUT PER RANK. For lattice-boundary hosts (𝒯_m: closed MCG, Deligne), every rationally constrained direction is handled by a face cut into its rational hull, a link lift, and a face lemma. The only new input per rank is a Diophantine shared-ray statement at totally irrational directions. That statement is free at very well approximable directions (a good approximation becomes a common ray, by Legendre capture), so the gate sits at Dirichlet-scale directions. Also: the plane lemma holds in every rank (bh-free-61 cut lemma), so Ore for 𝒪_E is LD_atom alone in every rank.
bh-free-09: For ANY SFT extension (base layer = skeleton/germs/flow, fibre = data/tiles) over any group and on spaces of any dimension, quantum rigidity ⟺ (C) base idempotents central in every quantum family ∧ rigidity of each fibre relative to a frozen classical base point (proof: localize the module over LC(base) at points; compactness recovers commutators). So the entire G2 content of every layered master-route design is centrality (C); base rigidity only makes base marginals commute with each other. Known sources of (C): fibre determinism, product groups; transplants/ABHT/simulations have neither — extension-rigidity-is-base-centrality-plus-pointwise-rigidity (4a734659e)
- bh-g1-universal-point [876c511de] free-products-with-z-inherit-m2-from-decidable-free-subshifts: the Bass–Serre tree of A*Z GLUES as well as minimizes. Library points sit at different vertices on a ruler schedule, so (M2) for A*Z needs only (D): a FREE subshift of A with DECIDABLE language. No minimality, recurrence or universal point is needed. Exposure lemma: fibers at scheduled positions are seen whole, so the library must be computable. E1's input side is now a pure effectivity question (a decidable Π⁰₁ free subshift). It is necessary for any SFT route, since minimal free SFTs are instances of it. Look for aperiodicity constraints with computable look-ahead, not recurrence schemes — decidable-groups-carry-free-subshifts-with-decidable-language.

Read with full bisections, the gluing kernel descends to closed invariant subsets with no freeness, and along quotients of the acting group, where only the labels r|_[0] (r ∈ R_0) are killed. So one object, the full shift A^(F_n) over the free group, decides P2′b and its exactness for every subshift over every n-generated group. The acting group's geometry and relators cannot help or hurt the gluing; they act only through V(Λ) and N_X. Refutations can be found in finite models: the first is whether V is the amalgam of an involution centralizer and a halving stabilizer.

- bh-g1-bss (fd33bb9f8, strongly-proximal-geometries-are-disjoint-from-measured-inputs): minimality of simulated SFTs has two enemies with opposite cures. Synchronization (BSS obstacle b) is a joining effect and vanishes when the geometry is strongly proximal (boundary of W_3 = Z/2*Z/2*Z/2 is a nearest-neighbour paradoxical SFT) and the input carries an invariant measure: every joining is the product. The floating locus is forced over EVERY countable group (injective codings with minimal product force finite type). So on Λ_1 x W_3 x W_3, gate E1 = (M2μ) + wall-data control, nothing else. Amenable (Z^2) geometries can never get this for free.
- bh-invent-16 [9823dcde1] minimal-system-automorphisms-fix-every-proximal-factor + subshift-automorphisms-are-outer-over-icc-scaffolds: CHEAP RIGIDITY AND COMMUTATION ARE EXCLUSIVE. Maps into proximal systems are unique, so an automorphism of a minimal system fixes every proximal factor and is determined by one point. Compression-rigid (proximal) SFTs have trivial Aut; almost automorphic, finite-over-proximal and principal-compact-over-skeleton ones have no m.a.p. automorphisms. Over ICC scaffolds, automorphisms meet neither the full group nor the crossed-product units. So RCA / Aut(σ) / time-lift hosts for Kazhdan simple inputs are dead over every rigid row SFT on main. They are not a bypass of gate U, since internalizing an automorphism puts the input in the acting group, i.e. (★′). The only live shape is a rigid, relatively weakly mixing SFT with a large commutant, and no rigidity source for weak mixing is known.
- bh-g2-buildings [bf61de7d0] tree-gluing-turns-rigid-sft-compactifications-into-rigid-sfts: MINIMALITY AND TOPOLOGICAL FREENESS ARE FREE; GLUE ALONG A LOCALLY INFINITE TREE. Over Λ_0 * Z, any quantum-rigid SFT compactification of Λ_0 (one isolated, dense seed orbit, trivial stabilizer, no dynamics of its own) becomes a minimal, topologically free, rigid SFT. Rigidity crosses the tree by the chain 1−A_i ≤ B_i ≤ 1−A_{i+1}. So E1′+E2 = (★𝒞): a rigid, locally checkable coordinate system with a unique origin on an fp overgroup. Canonical inputs are (FA) and simple, so they sit in one indecomposable member of 𝒞, and × and * never carry them.

For ANY Li-Garside category with trivial units and (Acyc) (no open set trapped beyond a loop ray), F(boundary groupoid x O_2) is F_infinity: Cuntz doubling makes every cylinder mergeable with its own copy, so packing >= piece count; (Acyc) gives the height. Non-commuting operator tilings double in scale along the operator Kakutani–Rokhlin tower, and recognizability puts the tower's corner projection into the child algebra, so commutation descends. J_D vanishes for all D >= max(r,1,⌈R/2⌉) or for none. Recognizable morphisms Ω_S → Ω_T push rigidity down (Ω_T rigid ⇒ Ω_S rigid), so one certificate can be transported along a chain. - bh-invent-03 [2e79547615] steinberg-fp-is-a-kakutani-invariant: full corners of fp algebras are fp (Morita), so the E2/(E′) gate, fp of the Steinberg algebra, is an invariant of the groupoid up to Kakutani equivalence: not of the coding, the transversal, or the acting group. - bh-free-57 (conway-amusical-permutation-is-the-full-three-shift, b8fa45c9d): coprime moduli = two stacks (Collatz/Conway map = baker map, so CT_P(Z) contains the full-shift full group and all RAAGs), but arithmetic hosts are diagonally rigid (one affine map on all prime coordinates): shift without a write head, V-attractors become odometer fibres. - bh-invent-13 [6a462d596] stabilizer-engines-embed-in-every-branch-compactification: an infinite emitter is the one-point compactification S⁺ of a branch set, and S⁺ is never an SFT. That is why finite-type finiteness engines (Li/Garside, BIM, Stein–Farley) stall there. Emitters sit at the bottom of a monotone lattice of blow-ups K, indexed by S-invariant C*-algebras between c_0(S)+C and ℓ^∞(S), with F_S ↪ F_S^K. So Reid's group is only the bottom test of Track C, and Track C and Track A′ are two ends of one family. For one-ended inputs (all Kazhdan) no finite-remainder blow-up exists, so the real gate is (IE′): an S-SFT with an isolated dense orbit (Z: step; Z²: corner SFT). Free remainders also take S out of isotropy, which lifts the (K′) warning.
- bh-invent-04 (e5b3fdf2e, tree-product-busemann-transplants-preserve-quantum-rigidity): TREES ARE TRANSPARENT TO RIGIDITY. Busemann transplant along any lattice in a product of trees (BMW, irreducible ones included, times any Λ_0) preserves and reflects both freeness and quantum rigidity of a cone-deterministic spacetime over Λ_0 × Z^r. The mechanism is chain centrality: each piece of a monotone arrow chain either freezes the far arrow or lets the tile step closer. So tree factors never create fibre rigidity. E2 over Λ_0 × (tree lattice) is exactly E2 for a free cone-deterministic spacetime over Λ_0 × Z^r. Only non-chain geometry (Ã2) could make a non-amenable factor do rigidity work.
- bh-free-54 [de6deaa2d] edge-split-order-is-not-geometric-refinement + edge-split-ore-iff-synchronization-and-tree-domination: in higher-rank refinement hosts the OPERAD order is strictly finer than geometric refinement, and the gap is exactly non-inherited descent (a 17-piece split tree respects x0=x1 but cannot split there first; it contains bh-free-61's depth-5 cell). So my LD_atom is FALSE; bh-free-61 must not rely on it. Correct split, every rank: Ore <=> Sync' + TD (tree domination: descendant cells that tile a cell can be organized into one split tree), and Ore <=> Ore against single atoms. Sync_m + TD also give saturation pi_1(O_E) = T_m (no Matui). Only Sync is dynamical, and TD is Garside-type completeness of reversing through flop squares. Test operad-order claims on deep witnesses, not small windows (7-piece exhaustive checks missed it).
- bh-invent-15 [b40a89028] hard-cusps-are-canonical-and-flux-free: geometry makes fp simple hosts only via boundary germs (finite geometric quotients stay acyl. hyperbolic). Hard inputs sit at cusps, every free cocompact cusp is (P x Cantor)^+, and for one-ended P (all simple inputs) germs commuting with P are only P-equivariant fundamental-domain changes (no flux). So relhyp gate (RB) and engine gate (IE) share one local model; Reid (P=Z) is the unique fluxed case, so the flux-free test is the engine of a one-ended fp decidable B (F, V).

- bh-kourovka-2146 [40650f1e7] commensurated-subgroups-of-virtually-simple-groups-are-rf + kourovka-21-46-reduces-to-simple-amalgams-of-davis-lattices: COMMENSURATED MEANS RESIDUALLY FINITE. The finite residual of a commensurated subgroup is normalized by the commensurator. So in a (virtually) simple host every proper (infinite-index) commensurated subgroup is RF: vertex groups of finite-index splittings, vertex stabilizers of locally finite tree/graph actions, slices of lattices in products. A non-RF input must be carried DIAGONALLY, by a non-commensurated subgroup with unbounded orbits on every locally finite factor. Consequences: three-tree lattices cannot import a 2-dim non-RF witness (Radu's Gamma_{4,4} trick has no 3-tree analogue); T x X_{O_d} can, through hyperplane stabilizers T x H. Torsion-free T x X lattices with edge-transitive tree action ARE amalgams W_1 *_A W_2 of Davis lattices (Neumann's F_m *_{F_k} F_n problem one dimension up, cd 3). The only non-local gate is extending a Burger-Mozes free amalgam from one hyperplane (a relative Leighton theorem, EXT).
- bh-kourovka-2174a [d61d20b6b] ct-z-residue-partition-question-finite-vs-infinite-reading: FINITE COORDINATES SEE COMPACT RECURRENCE; INFINITE ONES SEE ONLY GERMS. A dissipative element (fundamental domain plus bounded transit to a repeller) permutes a countable box partition for free. Box rigidity is forced only at recurrent points: germs of slope ≠ 1 at integers (τ_{1(2),0(4)}τ_{0(2),1(2)}), and for finite partitions also attractors in Ẑ∖Z (ψ). Every reduction on main puts its machine in a proper class, so it cannot see 21.74(a). Put a host's hard content in the germs at recurrent points; the wandering remainder is free.

A radius-1 reversible machine gets exact binary doubling, gap 12 and phi U^2 = U phi on hierarchical orbits (MSI, 15 levels), with no growing control information. Renormalization forbids periodic points, so every background the machine writes, its own counter letters included, must restart a hierarchy. Here b-blocks fail T for every fill explored (search incomplete). Design the junk first.
- bh-g2-buildings [ae9765a43] free-products-with-z-carry-minimal-topfree-effective-subshifts: ON THE TOPOLOGICALLY FREE ROUTE THE EXISTENCE GATE HAS NO EFFECTIVITY CONTENT. Gluing one-point compactifications along A*Z (the Bowditch boundary) gives, for every decidable A, a minimal topologically free effectively closed subshift with decidable language and no premise. (D) and universal points only buy FREENESS, which topfree routes do not consume. E1′ ⇐ MSS′ (BSS Q:minimal for topfree inputs) alone. - bh-openq-lists [ca38e5c7b] Branch groups are easy for BH and hard for residually finite hosts. The endomorphism that makes their L-presentation finite is realized for free by V's prefix shift and is forbidden in every RF host. So routes to BBMZ 5.3(12) must not factor through an fp RF overgroup of a branch group, and the Grigorchuk group is the first test of Rauzy's "corrected Higman theorem" for RF groups. In the same way, fp metabelian groups with a module of Krull dimension at least 2 do not serve as their own self-similar hosts (Kochloukova–Luiz, Kourovka 21.41). Hosts for both classes must forget the defining structure rather than extend it.

So in ANY seeded coding, E2 is exactly the question at infinite causal depth. paradoxical-subshifts-are-never-quantum-rigid: BSS geometry is free local choice, so never rigid. end-shift-path-covers-are-rigid-with-one-spine: a free factor's end flow dictates the paradoxical path cover rigidly, and its infinite-depth locus is ONE spine, over countably many ends.

## bh-ffwz-q59 (09-18 ~16:30): kernel removal is false in general (FFWZ Q5.9, both parts NO) — 25056ab91
- **Result.**
  - Scott's fp theorem for Röver–Nekrashevych groups never used faithfulness. V_d(G,ψ) is fp for any fp G and any recursion ψ.
  - With G = F_S and Bartholdi–Mitrofanov's table (arXiv:1710.10109, Thm A''), the clopen action is type [A_2] with a NON-RECURSIVE kernel.
  - SV of that action is an fp relatively simple group whose unique simple quotient has unsolvable WP.
- **Lesson.** A finite presentation plus an oligomorphic action can hide any co-r.e. kernel: a recursion table over a free group is the hiding place.
- **Consequences.**
  - '(relPBH)⇒(PBH)' and '(relBH)⇒(BH)' cannot go through general kernel removal.
  - v5 gate 6 (Q3.4 = kernel removal) is dead as a general statement. It survives only for the special actors it needs: the Theorem C actors Aut_Γ(Γ*F_n) ↷ Γ^n, where the kernel is mixed identities.
- **For hosts.** Finite presentation of Thompson-type recursion hosts is free. The whole difficulty is the tree kernel K_ψ, and V_d(Ḡ) is fp iff K_ψ is fng.
- bh-invent-07 [7a226daa54] thumann-finiteness-without-finite-type: FINITE TYPE IS A CONNECTIVITY DEVICE, NOT A FINITENESS HYPOTHESIS. Thumann uses it once, to bound the degrees of elementary classes in the corona. Replace it by (G)_n (the splitting category of every large elementary class is (n-2)-connected), and finitely generated operads with F_n transformations are F_n with ANY spine; finite presentation needs only connected splittings. Twisted BT grid spheres are (G). So infinite spines (closed-MCG edge-split host, Deligne) and unbounded-refinement operads (the only ones that beat SV_G, per bounded-refinement-operads-reduce-to-their-carriers) are not blocked; the gate becomes a local homotopy test on each elementary class.
- bh-emitter-a [580e4fd2a] stabilizer-engines-need-finitely-discriminable-vertex-groups: CONTINUITY SCHEMA. Suppose a host E(B) sees its input only through bounded local configurations (a transfer lemma along quotients B → B/N, restricting to π on B) and has a double commutator at an emitter ([[n,x],y] = [x,y] for almost all n ∈ B). Then E(B) fp forces B finitely discriminable. Finite presentation is open in marked-group space, so uniform one-emitter hosts only transport isolated groups. Reid (MO 339541) is not fp, the engine conjecture for fp decidable B is refuted, and lampshufflers over non-finitely-discriminable B are not fp. Found in parallel with free-product-emitter-full-groups-need-isolated-vertex-groups (stronger: isolation).
bh-synth-lead v6: ONE OBJECT. A seed SFT (isolated dense orbit, trivial stabilizer) = SFT compactification = finite-type branch compactification (IE′). A rigid seed on an fp overgroup is the only input-dependent premise of both routes; Tracks A′ and C are the same glued-seed construction (over Λ₀∗Z vs Z/2∗S), and Reid's C_Z blow-up is already Matui-fp. The decisive question is CAP: do rigid seeds cap the word problem? Build seeds by HNN gluing along seed-coded subgroups (BS(1,2), H₃, machine towers), and code (FA) geometry directly (Ã₂). — gq-bh-synthesis-master-route v6 (b5ad39956)
- bh-one-relator [29b6c4feb7] splitting-kills-the-finite-shadow-parity-of-the-gluing-group: FINITE SHADOWS ARE EXACT UP TO A LABEL-SUM CHARACTER, AND THOMPSON SPLITTING KILLS IT. For the two-point gluing test, the shadow over m cones is Sym(2m) × Z/2 (m = 3, 4, 5; GAP certificate), with Z/2 the parity of copy swaps. The doubling x = x_0 x_1, with x_0 and x_1 equal central involutions of a finer shadow, then forces every entangled braid to hold in the gluing group. Pattern: a finite computation makes the obstruction central, and the H_0(G_V) = 0 swindle kills it. What is left for gluing exactness is consistency across partitions, not local relations.
- bh-p2b-exact [commutation criterion] v-times-gluing-exactness-is-commutation-with-one-transposition: ONE TRANSPOSITION CONTROLS THE KERNEL. Any V×Λ gluing model is the normal closure of a single Thompson element (V simple, label swindle), and its kernel is relatively perfect. So the kernel is trivial iff it commutes with τ0=(00 01), and finitely normally generated iff finitely many relators force that commutation. Exactness of gluing presentations is a MONODROMY statement (lift τ0 around relator loops), and the only real work in FSG is one multiplicativity lemma for label-conjugated conditioned transpositions. Bounded factorizations (Γ~=MNM) are dead already over (Z,2).
- bh-invent-04 (5f79e3749, a2-busemann-transplants-preserve-quantum-rigidity): CYLINDER TRANSPORT settles centrality (C) for Ã2 (CMSZ, Kazhdan). A far skeleton event is a sum of sector cylinders at the tile's own site. Projective-plane uniqueness makes each cylinder the germ times a strictly smaller cylinder at every open-cone expansion site, so an induction on radius makes the skeleton central with no far commutation. So G2 in FREE form over one-ended Kazhdan lattices (and over tree lattices) reduces, through transplants, to ONE amenable question: is some strongly aperiodic cone-deterministic Z^2-SFT (Kari–Papasoglu) quantum rigid? Aim the rigidity effort there.
- bh-finf-hyp [62622d30a] bbmz-host-finiteness-reduces-to-the-vertex-boundary-groupoid: THE FREE FACTOR IS INERT. On the horofunction boundary of G*Z, every element is finitary off finitely many coset pieces (each ≅ ∂_h G), and groupoid homology in degrees ≥ 2 equals H_k(G; C(∂_h G)). Li plus Milnor–Moore give a cheap necessary test: the simple derived host is F_n only if H_k(G; C(∂_h G, Q)) is finite-dimensional for 2 ≤ k ≤ n. It passes for surface groups and in top degree for closed manifolds. It is OPEN in middle degrees (Kazhdan Sp(n,1) lattices = Čech cohomology of the horofunction suspension). The finite germ extension theorem cannot use either resolution of the parabolic vertex: the ∂_h resolution chops the singular pieces (V moves them), and the point resolution has a transport base that is not finitely generated (letter support). An F_∞ proof needs an intermediate resolution, or a germ theorem for non-atomic singular loci; this is gate (IE) with a hyperbolic input.

Keep the input B, but collapse a deep subgroup K into the base edge stabilizer (B *_K (K x Z/k)); no bounded pattern of the engine changes, yet k in K now fixes the base branch. One branch transposition then forces every deep subgroup to be normal, and with isolation there are none: fp engines need fp torsion vertex groups with finitely many prime-order elements, so F_V, F_T, F_F die too. Approximations see only kernels; collapses see all deep subgroups. BH must consume decidability through a non-local extension problem (aperiodic SFT over an fp carrier, Track A), never through local rules around the input.

A coded host sees g as a bisection only where codings from o and go agree after a finite prefix; for the pointed-cube coding that is exactly the regular Roller boundary R (tails beside a wall H keep a wrong last-cube flag forever for every g moving H across o). So a closed carrier must sit inside R. For Z^2*Z (a tree of flats) every closed invariant set contains the flat point (+inf,0), so no carrier exists: the pointed-cube+Cuntz route reaches exactly the cubulations with R closed (products of trees). Test any geometric coding for its resynchronization locus before building hosts; next coding must stratify by walls (hyperplane Roller boundaries, one dimension down).
