RSG host gates are finiteness problems for the pair (breakpoint module, slope group).
- bh-pal-kourovka61 (08e8495bd): a presentation of a full group by involutions (R1–R3 for CT_P(Z)) is exactly as hard as closing the Ore-category 2-cells. Every lift of a cellwise-canonical element is a transport, so the permutation and naturality relations are free and the kernel is generated by central, position-independent defects of the far-commutation and colour-exchange squares. The F_∞ answer to 17.61 is Li-only; the Bleak–Quick P=∅ proof uses Cannon–Floyd–Parry.
- [bh-invent-06b 25fe3dd25] LABEL DICHOTOMY + INFLATION CURE. In any groupoid whose germs carry a continuous Λ-label over an open map to X, every contracting loop is shadowed by some λ ∈ Λ with an attracting fixed point. So free, invariant-measure (so every amenable Λ) and distal X never have finitely coded labelled hosts: no passive enlargement of G_V×(Λ⋉X) works (skew products, fibres, Boolean powers, V-stabilization). Positive side: adjoining UNLABELLED inflation germs of a recognizable primitive box substitution makes translations+inflation a full contracting RSG (nucleus = one-level translations by {-1,0,1}^d across fault lines), fp with NO rigidity and NO finite type. Labbé's Wang shift qualifies, so E2 and free-P2′ infinite type are artifacts of the labelled host. Limit: contracting inflation needs an expanding endomorphism of Λ, hence virtually nilpotent Λ (Gromov). Over non-amenable directions, compression must be by Λ with isotropy (E3′) or by non-intertwining unlabelled germs (open; Conjecture SS T3).
- bh-free-56 [8bfdda46f, 85baba6fe] isolated-vertex-groups-have-fp-stabilizer-engines (recorded, then REFUTED by 58db3f3f9) + boone-higman-via-isolated-stabilizer-engines (DEAD): TRANSFER = LOCAL EMPTINESS + A CONFIGURATION-PRESERVING PROPER QUOTIENT. The nearby-group kill (Reid, isolation, collapse) needs exactly two things: (1) emptiness of Boolean combinations of basic sets is decided by bounded relations of the acting group (tree boundaries, full shifts: yes; SFTs and minimal subshifts: no, since extendability of a pattern is a global question); (2) a proper quotient of the acting group that preserves those bounded relations (quotients B/N for non-isolated B; amalgams B *_K (K x Z/2) for every B with deep K). Any fp host with a persistent monolith must break (1) or (2). Surviving hosts must make cylinder emptiness a GLOBAL question, which is the same place where Jeandel's argument consumes decidability. For Λ_1*Λ_2, K is normally generated by the factor kernels and ker(F(T_1) *_(LC(X,V)) F(T_2) → F(T)). Disjoint-support commutators die automatically in Γ~, because the trivial-label group pushes any two disjoint supports into complementary cones. Non-amenable T has a non-hyperfinite orbit relation, so there is no Brin–Thompson embedding (the Z route through 3V). The tree witnessing non-amenability replaces it.
- bh-near-actions [e8df48ee6] integral-slope-stein-v-groups-are-k-graph-full-groups + pisot-number-slope-stein-groups-are-contracting-rsgs: a Thompson-like host can carry several independent scalings only as COMMUTING renormalizations. Integral subdivisions commute, which is the k-graph unique-factorization axiom, so Li gives F_∞ (V_{2,3} is simple, F_∞ and type (A)). A single affine contracting coding carries exactly one Pisot S-unit slope: the finite nucleus is boundedness at every place but the coding place, and depth pays for the primes over β, so units are unnecessary. Independent slopes or non-Pisot slopes break the coding. Infinitely many independent scalings (GL_n(Q)) therefore need infinitely many commuting colours, or must be carried by the acting group.
- bh-invent-14 [b0fac81d0] decidable-free-subshifts-pass-to-graphs-of-groups: TREES ARE TRANSPARENT TO EFFECTIVITY. An end/root orientation plus a Thue-Morse colouring of Busemann heights kills every hyperbolic element of any tree action (infinite emitters included), with a finite hull check for extension. So E1 premise (D) passes through amalgams/HNN with decidable word+membership problems (GBS incl. non-RF BS(m,n), Burger-Mozes simple groups), and for (FA)/Kazhdan inputs it localizes to the vertex group holding the input: emitters obstruct finite type only, never existence; the open core of (D) is the (FA) case.
- bh-finf-hyp [horofunction-boundary-homology-reduces-to-the-branch-locus]: CANTOR CODINGS OF SPHERES FORCE WALLS. H^*(G; C(∂_h G, Q)) is the leftmost column of a descent spectral sequence over the branch locus B of ∂_h G → ∂G, converging to the cohomology of the boundary suspension (T^1M). B has totally disconnected complement, so dim B ≥ m−1 for S^m boundaries. For Sp(n,1) lattices the F_∞ homological test is a (4n−2)-dimensional tiling-cohomology problem. Finite atom types predict that it PASSES, so homology is unlikely to refute BBMZ Q1.2; the F_∞ gate is a germ theorem for non-atomic singular loci. No bounded computation can decide it.
- bh-g2-labbe [cc1e8896b] contextual-closure-certifies-quantum-rigidity: FAULT SLABS BLOCK DETERMINISM, NOT RIGIDITY. Case splits over a commuting context, where each case determines ONE end, cross fault normals. They are blocked only by double faults, and a double fault inside a window is an explicit 2-dim representation of the window algebra, which excludes every certificate there at any degree. Labbé at scale 2: determinism certifies exactly B_4; context splits cross both axis faults at distance 5 (26/40 offsets). At the golden-resonant (6,0) certificates are provably non-local (height >= 13), matching ‖6 − (φ−1)‖ = 0.146. So E2 for toral codings is an induction along the continued fraction of the rotation, and the diagonal faults are the current frontier. Gröbner on C_2 cannot terminate; use window algebras.
- bh-g1-bss (c797680f6, keyed-slots-bypass-the-phase-field-on-boundary-geometry): CORRECTION of 4ff61dec5's 'single shape' claim. The forced plane-varying floating datum is DATA, not geometry: keyed slots (695a89096) let each plane pick its own floating identities keyed by its own table, and freeness of the input makes keys distinct, which diversifies every tuple. So E1 needs no phase field, measure or boundary geometry. Boundary planes W_3^2 matter only if later gates want non-amenable hierarchy directions; there minimal lifts are automatically topologically free, and full freeness is the one extra gate.
- bh-invent-15 [c90fc9348] seeds-never-survive-marked-approximation: over an fp group a seed pattern has NO configuration over any proper marked approximant (pull back along the quotient, then uniqueness + trivial stabilizer kill the kernel). A seed = relative isolation, and for seeded groups this is equivalent to fp; it replaces finite discriminability. So approximation obstructions (which kill emitters) can never touch seed engines, and CAP needs a non-local (word-problem) argument. Open: does fp + seed force a solvable WP?
- bh-invent-13 [CP 2001 at source]: Coornaert–Papadopoulos's 'horofunctions form an SFT' is a ONE-SIDED SFT for the single map α (step to the smallest descending generator), with local-to-global along the tube of one gradient ray. It is not a Γ-SFT, and its horofunctions (distance-like to horospheres, no minimum) contain the limits of point-distance functions but are not known to equal them. So the seeded two-sided statement (HL: R-locally distance-like ⇒ limit of distance functions) is genuinely open. Lesson: 'X is an SFT' in the literature often means a Z-coding along rays; check which group acts before importing it into a seed or compactification argument.
bh-invent-11 [c4710d9ab2] machine-labelled-lamp-products-are-nv-centralizers: ONE HOST COMPILES, AND IS CAPPED. For any K ≤ n'V, one detector in (n'+O(1))V realizes the product over Z of centralizer subgroups of K chosen position by position by an NLIN_1 labelling. Mechanism: lamps ride on untouched coordinates, branches emit labels at time C·s, the detector applies the label on the lamp coordinates. So identity-edge permanence over machine-coded subgroups is free in B_A. CAP side: time per lamp index is linear and detector tests are coNP, so stages compiled in one fixed host stay in one class. Beating a cap needs hosts that vary with the input or unbounded time per index.
- bh-p2b-exact [96d92cb72] cantor-gluing-kernel-is-the-union-of-finite-alphabet-kernels: EXACTNESS IS FINITARY AND ROBUST; FSG IS NOT. The Cantor-alphabet gluing kernel is the directed union of the finite-alphabet kernels, each a retract. Together with descent (one-relator), free-product splitting (invent-16) and the commutation criterion, all of P2′b over every f.g. group reduces to FPG plus: for Z acting on each 2^n-letter full shift, lifting τ0 around relator loops has trivial monodromy. Central defects are already dead (finite-shadow parity, swindle). The one remaining statement is consistency of label-conjugated transposition lifts across non-symmetric partitions.
- bh-free-54 [6449ca40f] edge-split-descendant-dissections-are-tree-dominated Attempt 4: tree domination = termination of a top-down cut-and-synchronize recursion, and the SPLIT RULE decides everything. Least damage (minimise straddlers and non-inherited fragments) terminated on every test in ranks 3 and 4: at most 7 cuts, NO synchronization ever, leaves <= 1.83x pieces; it reproduces 227db96ee's 2-cut hand repair. Following one fragment's descent path (the Garside normal-form instinct) diverges (1.8e5 cuts). Lesson for host finiteness proofs: repairs are local and bounded when each move is chosen to minimise disturbance, so look for a straddle-counting potential, not a normal form.
- bh-outfn-2 [6f601b9ce] boundary-pushout-of-aut-out-free-splits-mod-every-quotient: SIMPLE-KERNEL PUSHOUTS ARE QUOTIENT-BLIND. Push an extension into a kernel M with D(M) simple and perfect (any purely infinite minimal full group): every proper quotient of M is abelian, so every quotient-level obstruction collapses to the abelianized extension (for Aut→Out it splits virtually). Nilpotent/Johnson/Fox obstructions cannot touch the pushed problem. What remains is dynamical: a strict model with a finite orbit IS a virtual section, and every point stabilizer lifts through germs. So hosts are refuted only by fixed-point/germ arguments and built only by spreading the section problem over infinite stabilizers of a Cantor action.
- bh-partials [e635172c96] self-similar-interval-numerations-force-perron-slopes: PERRON-FROBENIUS IS THE BARRIER FOR SELF-SIMILAR HOSTS. A finite self-similar interval numeration with ratios in ⟨λ⟩ forces 1/λ weak Perron (finite places: algebraic integer; infinite places: dominant conjugate). So λ=2/3 and transcendental slopes are beyond every coding method; Perron but not Pisot slopes land on BBMZ's non-contracting question Q1.4.
- bh-invent-16 [114480fcd] free-product-gluing-equals-splitting-of-the-gluing-kernel: FOR FREE PRODUCTS, GLUING EXACTNESS = FREE-PRODUCT GLUING. Γ~(Λ_1*Λ_2) is exactly the amalgam of the factor gluing groups over LC(X,V) mod label commutation. So FPG ⇔ the kernel splits over the factors, exactness forces FPG, and FPG holds when the action preserves fibres (trivial actions). The whole open content over F_n is EX(Z) on the Cantor-alphabet shift plus coherence of "type-changing" elements (a clopen carried by one factor, its complement by the other) in the Boolean power of the Bass–Serre tree.
- bh-invent-17 [v-times-subshift-full-group-finiteness-is-horizontal]: FINITENESS IS HORIZONTAL. For V × any topologically free subshift, descending links of the domain-filtered Stein space are (n−2)-connected in every level and every degree. The swap lemma gives unique maximal joint expansions, so each level is a Stein space. Hatcher–Vogtmann on disjoint minimal merges gives the core, and Thumann's corona-from-core estimate applies because block sizes are bounded per level, so (G) is vacuous. Hence F_n ⟺ the domain filtration is essentially (n−1)-connected. E3′/P2′ = "loops at bounded domain radius fill at a larger bounded radius"; rigidity is its linear shadow. No link, packing or height condition remains.
- bh-emitter-a [6991cc858] heisenberg-rigid-seed-certifies-only-the-malcev-normal-form: IN A RIGID SEED, COMPLEXITY ENTERS ONLY THROUGH DISTORTION. The H3 seed is the sign pattern of its Malcev coordinates. Forcing radius is linear and forcing length is quadratic (the centre distortion); it certifies the logspace normal form. Seeds from locally finite splittings are pointers plus one sink, so they certify only Britton normal forms over the sink. A CAP test (rung 1c) needs relative seeds over computation-distorted infinite-index subgroups, and CAP should be stated for forcing length.
bh-free-35 [dc1c8cfb5]: The bounded-range certificate search (Singular letterplace, D=1, two 1-core MSI jobs) is sound and calibrated. It certifies Ledrappier and a planted local-coordinate rigid SFT at distance 3, certifies nothing for the full shift, and on the colour product stops exactly at the 8 wall pairs. So at small scale it separates determinism from walls exactly; its reach beyond determinism is untested (no unconditionally rigid Z^2 SFT with a two-sided fault is recorded), and 19-tile Labbe needs a structure-aware engine — gq-bh-free-35-certificate-search-2026-09-18.
- bh-major-mcg-2 [f0d690dc4, e120be82f] (G) CALIBRATION: the smallest edge-split spine elements (flop pairs, degree 5) have splitting category S^0, two-sided like a two-direction full split. So condition (G) for the closed-MCG operad asks exactly whether LARGE spine elements are many-sided. TI GATE: at directions whose flow orbit recurs (almost every direction), shared rays follow from one tracking lemma (descent systems contain bounded-distortion copies of the fat cell at recurrence times) plus a finite synchronization check; singular directions are where to look for a counterexample.
- bh-invent-03 [5deee01840] relative-seeds-glue-graphs-of-groups-into-class-c: the (SEED) gate is LOCAL TO SUBGROUPS. A rigid relative seed for (A,C) is an isolated point with stabilizer exactly C, i.e. a locally forced marking of one C-coset. These glue along ANY graph of groups (with atoms, exclusivity and monotone chains making the Bass–Serre tree free) and survive finite-index induction. They exist for every fg subgroup of free and free abelian groups, so every graph of free/free abelian groups with fg edge groups is in 𝒞, including HNN extensions of free groups such as modular-machine groups (the first rung of an Aanderaa–Cohen tower). The forced point is a membership oracle for C. CAP = can a rigid relative seed encode a hard membership problem? Test (K_M, R_0).
- bh-invent-14 [41501ac60] higman-clapham-envelopes-are-hnn-over-machine-times-input: A HIGMAN ENVELOPE HAS ONE COUPLING. With a benign pair, P = HNN_s(machine x input) and machine meets input only in graph(F -> G). So (D), seeds and rigidity each split into input + machine + one HNN along graph(pi); in seed form emitters cost exactly one seed-coded subgroup pair; CAP is decided at the envelope by whether local rules can write the graph of the quotient map (premise (DB) for a total-decider tower unverified).
bh-free-09: Quantum rigidity is NOT closed under fibre products over the SAME group: Ledrappier and its mirror image are rigid, but their product over Z^2 has opposite one-sided configurations, hence walls (first explicit failure of (C) with both layers rigid and the fibre rigid at every base point). One-sided freedom in direction -v = no rules in the +v half-space; a site transport closure K = Λ (one layer's rules cover the other's one-sided freedom) certifies (C) over any group. Design rule: give all layers of a G2 construction a common open determinism cone (why the bh-invent-04 transplants work) — quantum-rigidity-is-not-closed-under-fibre-products (34f57977b)
- bh-one-relator [f040a4fa61] two-point-gluing-presentation-of-v-is-exact: GLUING IS EXACT ONCE CANONICAL LIFTS ARE NATURAL. V_(2,2) = V(Z/2) *_(V×Z/2) (V≀Z/2) / ⟨⟨[M_0,N_1]⟩⟩, so the smallest P2′b test is exact. Define a lift of every transposition (cross lifts between comparable cones by moves). Check naturality under each generator of both pieces, a finite case analysis from (R1), (R2) and finite-shadow braids. Strong naturality then gives the conjugacy relations, and Bleak–Quick gives a section. This is the multiplicativity lemma of bh-p2b-exact's criterion, in its simplest instance. For Cantor Λ-spaces the new case is naturality of conditioned lifts under local labels of infinite order.

## bh-ffwz-q59 pass 2 (09-18 ~17:00): where Theorem C kernel removal is actually needed
- Burger-Mozes and Kac-Moody lattices and all Cantor-dense hosts are MIF, so their Theorem C kernel is trivial. Nothing is removed there.
- After the Q5.9 counterexample, the BH=>PBH residue along Theorem C is exactly BFFHZ Q3.2 for non-MIF fp simple groups, which are 1-dimensional, circle-type hosts. The first test is T.
- Obstruction type. Theorem C kernels are recursive iff mixed identities are, and they are recursive for T. So a NO for Q3.2 needs a NON-FINITELY-BASED J, not undecidability. A YES for T would come from the rigidity heuristic: breaking cyclic order anywhere breaks it everywhere.
- No recorded fp simple group blocks BH=>PBH. Every non-MIF one known lies in V, and hence in PBH, by other means.
- 2026-09-18 bh-ref-engines: the engine obstructions (isolation + collapse transfer) pass review; the shared transport only ever needs 'empty upstairs ⇒ empty downstairs', which survives any collapse, so check which direction of a transfer lemma a proof actually uses before trusting (or distrusting) its counting.
- bh-g1-universal-point [f6aedfd93] bs12-rigid-seed-is-its-normal-form: on a locally finite tree a rigid seed IS the normal form written as a local rule: a pointer to the origin plus the sign of one coordinate. BS(1,2)'s seed has 6 letters and is rigid for D ≥ 2 by two monotone chains. It is forced at LINEAR radius despite exponential distortion, because balls contain exponentially long a-segments. It carries exactly WP, which is in P for all GBS groups. So locally finite rungs are CAP-neutral. Hardness can enter a seed only via (i) the vertex seed, (ii) compressed arithmetic in a distorted vertex group (is P closed under finite-index HNN when CWP(A) ∈ P?), or (iii) relative seeds over infinite-index edges, which is where Higman/SBR towers live.
- bh-free-57 (brin-thompson-nv-needs-n-minus-one-primes-in-ct-z, 7a141a647): k-graph leaves have asdim <= k (the degree map has ultrametric fibres), so by Matte Bon a rank-k host holds nV only if n <= k. A copy of nV in CT(Z) needs n-1 odd primes (3V is not in CT_{3}(Z)), and at the threshold every copy is spatial. Writer questions reduce to explicit commuting Cuntz families of the host's partial maps, a finite object to search for or exclude.
- bh-g2-fixedpoint-a (4f793f5c3c, crossing-wire-fixed-point-tile-sets-are-quantum-rigid): RIGIDITY IS MANUFACTURED BY CO-LOCATION. Raw bits commute once copies meet in one tile (crossing), and deterministic gates spread commutation. A fixed-point tile set with complete crossing, a deterministic zone and side-local margins is quantum rigid and aperiodic. E2 over Z^2 now reduces to MINIMALITY of such a shift.
bh-invent-11 [5ecd29e1b4] compiled-hnn-stages-are-capped-inside-brin-thompson-groups: THE ONE-HOST CAP IS A BRIN–THOMPSON CAP. Every fg subgroup of every nV has coNP word problem (Birget, recalled). So iterating the in-host compiler never beats coNP, even if each identity-edge HNN stage lands back in some nV. "Embeds back" is a subgroup-class question, and in V it already fails (Z²∗Z, Bleak–Salazar-Díaz, recalled). CAP rung 1c therefore reduces to hard type (A) actors (twisted Brin–Thompson hosts), not to compilers.
- bh-free-61 [e0337551b4] half-tube-lattice-points-are-edge-split-vertices + unimodular-cells-synchronize-under-edge-splits-proof: FREE-CHOICE SERRET IS SOLVED BY A SHARED VERTEX, NOT A SHARED TAIL. In rank >= 3, every lattice point within sup-distance 1/2 of a generic ray (in a cell's coordinates) is a descent vertex: among >= 3 rounding errors two share a sign, and that pair is always a legal edge split that keeps the tube. Dirichlet puts one point in both cells' tubes. The link at the shared vertex has rank one lower, and points on rational subspaces split into a generic problem inside the subspace and a full problem in the quotient. So (Sync_m) holds for EVERY m, with no global potential. The pointwise entry-sum potential is provably insufficient: 67 of 1369 stuck matrices fail it. Consequence: for the edge-split operad, Ore = tree domination alone, in every rank (MCG route R1). Transfer: any host whose cells are unimodular simplices (nV with GL germs, 2V_Lambda, Lagrangian, train-track split hosts): look for a reachability tube for the free-choice subdivision.
- [bh-invent-02, a5f6a15a5] higman-closure-of-abelian-cofinite-hosts-is-port-faithfulness: every Higman operation on hosts (port amalgams, HNN, free products) is free EXCEPT faithfulness, whose failure is the free Bass-Serre kernel of FFWZ Q5.9. So relPBH-universality and Q3.4 meet at one gate: faithful gluing along a cofinite port. Cofinite ports have virtually abelian centralizers, so Sym(N)-style generic gluing is unavailable; faithfulness must come from Cantor-type ping-pong (the odometer host V_(2,1)(<a>) is a genuine host).
- 09-18 bh-ref-ffwz: FFWZ Q5.9 NO (25056ab91) passes review. Part 2 still rests on fp of non-faithful SV_G (Thm B), so referee abstract-twisted-bt-type-fn-criterion next. Lesson: a finite recursion table over a free group is a free fp host for any co-r.e. kernel, so non-faithful Thompson-like actors carry no computability content by themselves.
- bh-pal-kourovka61 (2bde1f5a1): with comb frames, far commutation in the transposition presentation of CT_P(Z) closes exactly modulo the Thompson V-core. Given the Ore presentation and Bleak–Quick, the whole kernel is one central class per colour pair (the H_2 shape of the k-graph groupoid), which the colour with two edges should kill via a {2,p,q}-cube swindle.
- bh-invent-15 [a9187d6d4] seeded-groups-have-solvable-word-problem: recursively presented + seed (isolated point, trivial stabilizer; NO rigidity, NO density) => decidable WP. This is Jeandel localized to the seed cylinder: w != 1 iff seed + rules + (w=1) is finitely inconsistent over F/<<Rel,w>>. So (SEED) consumes exactly the decidability Clapham supplies, and CAP = a uniform bound on the seed-collapse (contradiction-size) function kappa.
- bh-kourovka-2174a [4267a301f] ct-z-residue-partition-certificates-live-on-s-adic-coordinates: SKEW-PRODUCT PRINCIPLE. Every element of an arithmetic host CT_P(Z) is finite-type dynamics on the active coordinates Ẑ_S, with affine unit fibre maps on the passive Ẑ_{S′}. The passive coordinates cannot compute; structure on them is confined to where the cocycle is trivial; certificates on them are free (a generic fibre point plus one ideal condition). Reduce coordinate and rigidity questions to the active part plus a cocycle ideal. A 21.74(a) undecidability proof needs an undecidable torsion problem, because finite order is the only YES source.
- bh-outfn-2 [0c67a8335] piecewise-representative-lifts-are-rigid-under-cheap-moves: A BAND'S SECTION PROBLEM IS RIGID UNDER EVERY CHEAP MOVE. Commensurability changes nothing. Profinite blow-ups buy freeness (hence every germ constraint) only via an equicontinuous coordinate, and a lift there descends to a finite level, so it exists iff a finite-index lift does. Splittings are unavailable for Kazhdan inputs (FA). Before building a host for a group with a band, ask for FINITE INTEGRAL COORDINATES on an honest compact action: Mod(S_g) has them (PML train tracks ⇒ pure finiteness problem, 𝒯_m), Out(F_n) has none known (Smillie–Vogtmann: no finite length coordinates on CV_n) ⇒ strictification + finiteness. Open: a faithful piecewise-integral-projective action of Out(F_n) on a compact polyhedron would import the whole 𝒯-machinery.
- bh-gln-tracka [9d121fa9cc] end-shift-codings-of-q-inputs-need-fp-rf-semilocal-vertex-groups: A TREE BOUNDARY IS AN SFT EXACTLY WHEN STABILIZERS ARE FG, AND THEN THEY MUST BE FP AND RF. The end shift of a cocompact locally finite tree with fg vertex stabilizers is an SFT over the acting group (vertex-frame rules + edge rules), minimal and topologically free whenever the boundary action is, so on the group route it bypasses M2. But topological freeness makes vertex groups RF, and finite presentation makes them fp. So the input part that the tree cannot move (its (FA) part, or its divisibility beyond the valence) must sit in an fp RF vertex group. That is impossible for Kazhdan inputs without finite quotients, and for (Q,+)-inputs it is the semilocal form (SL) of the fp-RF Z_(l) bottleneck, now its fourth consumer.
- bh-near-actions [8c548b3da] oligomorphic-actions-with-f-n-set-stabilizers-are-f-n: the fp clause of type (A) need not be proved separately. If the action has finitely many orbits on sets of size ≤ 3 and those setwise stabilizers are fp, then the actor is fp (Brown's criterion on the full simplex over one orbit), and the action is type (A). Circle groups are the model case: 3-set stabilizers are F³ ⋊ Z/3, so T_{2,3} and T_τ are F_∞. Design BH actors so that finite-set stabilizers split into copies of one known F_∞ piece.
- [bh-g2-fixedpoint-b, 4c2d76fe4] seeded-transport-codings-over-end-flows-are-quantum-rigid: v6's 'monotone chains toward the seed' in operator form. Downhill induction: a flow step away from the target forces the target's letter. So seeded transport codings (bijective in space, any function in time) are rigid on every module, and the spine sheet carries at most |C| global choices. Functional phase fields are rigid, but (heuristically) not minimal. The E1-and-E2 crux on squares is a RELATIONAL phase coupling that stays unsplittable.
- bh-finf-hyp [af235c6d8] configurations + singular blocks: (1) CONFIGURATIONS ARE AS FINITE AS ATOMS. Bounded tuples of hyperbolic atoms meeting one boundary point have finitely many types (thin triangles plus the BBMZ signature argument), so all fiber products of ∂_h G over ∂G are finite-type coded. (2) GERM THEORY WORKS FOR BLOCKS, BUT FULL GROUPS HAVE NO BLOCKS. The BHM finite germ theorem holds verbatim with singular closed blocks. A full group containing V admits no nontrivial blocks. Block-preserving groups over G-cosets have infinitely generated transport bases, because only the vertex group moves branch letters. So a hard input is absorbed only by a global complex. The hyperbolic F_∞ gate is now Thumann's connectivity (G) for the shadow operad (all partitions into H-translates of shadows), checked over finitely many configuration types.
- bh-openq-lists [6dc3f9d10] Thompson's F cannot be created by extensions or directed unions (a copy of F in G lies in N or in G/N, since every nontrivial normal subgroup of F contains F', which contains F). So anything built from residually finite pieces by extensions and ascending HNN extensions, which is how branch groups become finitely presented, is F-free with no amenability input. Kourovka 21.140 and Zaremsky 2.8 are now pure finiteness questions (FP_∞ of the Basilica HNN extension).
- bh-g2-buildings [1fa63700d] k-local-distance-functions-on-hyperbolic-groups-are-global: on δ-hyperbolic Cayley graphs, (2δ+4)-local distance patterns force a unique sink (a global distance function) or a unique centre with (2δ+4)-fellow-travelling descents, i.e. an integral Coornaert–Papadopoulos horofunction. So SFT compactifications of hyperbolic groups hinge on ONE tube lemma, Φ_0 ⊆ ∂_h (CP horofunctions are limits of distance functions). Merging of descents is sufficient; a failure lives in a bounded tube around one ray.
- bh-invent-13 [9c07701aa] horofunction-lemma-holds-for-free-and-surface-groups: local-to-global for horofunctions is a HELLY statement. A locally realizable choice of halfspaces is globally realizable once the halfspace system has a Helly number and minimal obstructions have bounded size, and negative curvature bounds them (separating walls shorten pointing-away pairs; hyperbolic trigonometry bounds the rest). So every closed surface group has an SFT compactification with a dense seed orbit: the first one-ended (IE′) examples beyond products. For Kazhdan inputs, which have no walls (FW), the Helly-type halfspace system must be built from horospheres or cone types.
- bh-p2b-exact [02399e478] incomparable-transposition-lifts-are-equivariant-off-self-swaps: INCOMPARABILITY IS FREE, COMPARABILITY IS THE WHOLE PROBLEM. In any G_V × H gluing model, lifts of transpositions between incomparable cones commute when disjoint and are equivariant under the labelled Thompson piece and under every conditioned swap except self-swaps. A spare cone reduces every swap to straddles, which (R1) and (R2) compute. The only possible failure is created when a label moves P off itself inside ONE cone ([w]×P ↔ [w]×λP). Exactness of P2′b over every group and subshift = one coherence statement (CL): the self-swap formula for comparable lifts must be independent of the auxiliary cone. Partial self-swaps recurse toward the partner-map fixed point, the same contraction phenomenon as elsewhere.
- bh-ref-misc (09-18, 601a0b497f): The claim "product closure and (JE) for every recorded fp simple group" is exactly as strong as three unreviewed finite-presentation inputs: Khanh; K-vanishing of L_p^(⊗4), where the ρ-doubling pushes the odd-p case into degree 4; and W_(P,ℓ) f.p. Doubling tricks move a claim to a higher-degree host, so check that the inputs were audited at that degree.
- [bh-invent-06b d48697d27] FAULTS ARE THE WHOLE CONTENT OF V-STABILIZING INFLATION; χ≠0 KILLS ENDOMORPHISM INFLATION. F(G_V × inflation groupoid) is F_∞ when there are no faults (Z^d odometer: relaxed Thumann, transformations Z^d, spine = halving/digit-split/grid). With faults the translations become rational maps with one level of lag, so no known 2-dimensional engine (Brin/Li/Thumann) applies. The BBMZ nucleus becomes infinite in the product coding, so the missing tool is a product theorem for contracting RSGs (OPEN node v-times-inflation-host-full-groups-are-finitely-presented). Over F₂×F₂, and any VF group with χ≠0, a finite-index injective endomorphism is an automorphism, so no endomorphism inflation exists, expanding or not. Unlabelled compression there must be a non-coset hierarchy (quasi-isometric tree supertiles) or seed-type. Otherwise use E3′ (compression by Λ with isotropy) or mixed Λ_c × Z^j designs.
- bh-ref-kourovka-b [4697bc8f58] pbh-class-closed-under-graph-products + ct-p-z-is-a-one-vertex-k-graph-full-group, referee PASS: B_A CLOSURE = ONE FAITHFUL MAP INTO Aut_H(H*F_2). Free letters carry free products, and a twisted conjugation supplies whatever commutes with them, so retract-plus-commuting constructions stay in the class. CT_P(Z) is F_infinity via Li cor:OneVertex with Y = X (e_P empty).
- bh-e3prime [58fb2e8438] faithful-finite-index-hnn-upgrades-to-half-tree-free: TOPOLOGICAL FREENESS COSTS ONE FACTOR OF Z; THE COMPLEXITY GATE IS INTRINSIC. A Baumslag–Solitar twist on A×Z turns any faithful finite-index HNN extension (ascending allowed) into a non-ascending one with no element fixing a half-tree, at the same complexity. So three open nodes are one question: (SS-hard)_1, attempt 5 of the fp-simple complexity root (via Bux–Llosa Isenrich–Wu), and the finite-index case of hard self-similar groups. Product and wreath tricks do not help by themselves: the hard group must itself admit a commensuration that shrinks every normal subgroup. KMS test: Tor(G(M)) is locally finite with G/Tor = Z^{4K}. Such a commensuration would be a renormalization symmetry of the machine's configuration module, and every shape available from the presentation (Frobenius, Cartier, equivariant scaling) is dead. Next: Σ^1 of the metabelian layer, which should decide the expected homothety rigidity.
- bh-solvable [399d8c45f, 3bd169bc8] levelled-actions-make-finitary-extensions-not-fp2: the lampshuffler obstruction needs only a LEVEL, not freeness. If a virtual character vanishes on the point stabilizers of one orbit, FSym(X) x| G is not FP_2 over Q. Any two split trees of a dissection are joined by local moves at minimal common upper bounds of two atoms, and only a move covering the whole dissection disconnects. So a splitting category is connected iff the root graph is. Two-sidedness lives only at the first spine level, and condition (G) for finite presentation of non-LCM Thompson-like hosts (MCG, Deligne) is a question about PAIRS of moves, not about the whole spine.

Template: finite local state + promise moves + Cuntz letter + free exits.

- bh-typeA-universal: Saturation is free. If a carrier group Λ acts by fractions of a symmetric Ore refinement operad whose pieces are identified by Λ-local maps, then the operad group IS [[Λ]]. - bh-star-b [f676c07d2] rigid-subshifts-over-free-factors-are-tight-almost-everywhere + tree-lattice-homogeneous-subshifts-are-not-quantum-rigid: **OVER A FREE FACTOR, RIGIDITY BUILDS ITS OWN BOUNDARY.**
  - Mechanism: the free branches of nested slabs converge to an equivariant end.
  - (RA) is a theorem over F_n: every rigid subshift without isolated points factors continuously onto ∂F_n, so its action is amenable.
  - Over Λ0×F_n, invariant measures live on tight points, i.e. points fixed by one slab.
  - Mozes-type homogeneous spaces Γ\(H1×H2) are measure-preserving and never rigid: compact stabilizers of slab states rotate all branches at once, like an emitter's isotropy.
  - So boundary dynamics are rigid and amenable, homogeneous dynamics are neither. The only place left for a measure-preserving (RA) counterexample over Λ0×F_n is a tight spacetime.
- bh-star-a [e3689f09a] quantum-rigidity-passes-to-subshifts + torsion-free-tree-product-lattices-lie-in-the-rigid-seed-class: CARVING IS FREE. Rigidity passes to subshifts, so a rigid seed = a seed carved from ANY rigid ambient, and CAP (rigid) asks only what rigid ambients can carry. Every known ambient-builder is complexity-neutral: tree/splitting (Britton, bh-g1-universal-point), deterministic finite-fibre layers (finite transducers; κ only drops), and CAT(0) geometry. Tree-product lattices, incl. Burger–Mozes simple groups, are in 𝒞, capped at NP. ¬CAP needs a layer rigid by its own determinism, coupled to compression and carved at an origin.
- bh-invent-10 [f06cf5b6f] seeded-recursively-presented-groups-have-solvable-word-problem: A SEED IS A FINITE PROOF OF ITS OWN UNIQUENESS. Over a recursively presented group, the seed point is a Π^0_1 singleton, hence computable, and trivial stabilizer makes w = 1 iff the seed pattern reappears at w^-1. So fp + seed (no rigidity) ⇒ solvable WP and decidable language, one uniform algorithm; CAP is only about the refutation depth of that search.
- bh-invent-10 [f06cf5b6f] quantum-rigidity-is-decided-on-the-derived-subshift: THE ORIGIN IS CLASSICAL. The idempotent of a locally forced isolated point is central and acts as the point evaluation in every quantum family. So Y is rigid iff its derived subshift is: a locally forced seed is rigid iff ∂Y is, and locally forced countable subshifts of finite CB rank are rigid for free. The word problem is paid at the origin (forcing), rigidity and minimality on the perfect kernel (where free layers are fatal, 7ac816a5c).

- bh-invent-13 [807e18d5d] roller-compactifications-of-cube-complexes-are-sfts: for median geometries the horofunction lemma is free. 2-ball local realizability forces an ultrafilter (a one-step descent to adjacent hyperplanes, then Helly number 2), so the Roller compactification of any free cocompact cube complex IS an SFT with isolated principal seeds. - bh-g1-bss (74597e3ce, decidable-groups-times-z-have-decidable-free-subshifts + route minimal-free-sft-overgroups-via-storage-column-free-subshifts): decidability of an extension problem comes from INDEPENDENCE OF SCALES. One storage Z gives each point a private Toeplitz column; each scale's freeness witness (maximal r_k-net + proper coloring of its 2r_k+k graph, spare colors) sits on its own rows and is greedily completable with a one-annulus look-ahead. So (D) holds for A x Z for every decidable A, and with 876c511de (tree gluing) and 695a89096 (keyed slots) gate E1 is closed modulo review: K ⊆ ((Λ_1 x Z) * Z) x Z^2 with a minimal free SFT. Weight now on E2/E3.
bh-free-35 [47f947ad2]: A determinism-rigid free Z^2 SFT needs an IRREVERSIBLE TIME ARROW across every rational line. In each rational direction it is the spacetime of a surjective strip automaton, non-injective exactly at the non-expansive lines, on an aperiodic, non-sofic strip subshift of at least quadratic complexity (Cyr-Kra). Products of Z-subshifts, including product substitution tilings, always have two-sided faults: an infinite Z-subshift has asymptotic pairs on both sides (Morse-Hedlund). Kari-Papasoglu corner sets qualify only if an axis seed is forced. So self-simulation must run inside a Ledrappier-type irreversible rule — no-two-sided-fault-sfts-have-rich-strips-in-all-directions.
- bh-g2-fixedpoint-a (49939b957a, minimal-crossing-wire-fixed-point-shift-is-quantum-rigid): CLASSICAL GADGETS ARE FREE. Frame-forced gadgets (DR diversification slots, coordinate-fixed fields) are scalars in every quantum family. So minimality, and plausibly other dynamical upgrades, costs no rigidity. Result: a free MINIMAL quantum-rigid Z^2 SFT (lane proof, under review). E2 now needs transport to non-amenable Lambda.
- bh-finf-hyp [hyperbolic-cone-matching-holds-off-the-branch-locus]: HYPERBOLICITY MAKES ELEMENTS FINITARY WHERE THE CANTOR MODEL IS INJECTIVE. Off the branch locus of ∂_h G → ∂G, deep atoms map onto atoms, because d(g,·) − d(1,·) is eventually constant on the front of the ball. So for hyperbolic full groups the finitely generated nucleus-tile operad gets Thumann's (VE) for free, and square filling fails only over branch points. It holds if the non-surjective part of the nucleus automaton has no cycle (C), a finite decidable test. Under (C) the spine is finite and (G) is not needed, so the F_∞ half of Zaremsky's question reduces to (C) plus color-tameness. The all-partitions shadow operad is the wrong engine: its (VE) is the same cone-matching problem in disguise.
- bh-major-mcg-2 [a65f9528b] TRACKING = BOUNDED RESIDUE: the descendants of c that contain a fat cell φ are exactly the stages of left reduction of W_c^{-1}Φ, so tracking needs one reduction order with bounded residue. - [bh-invent-02, a52e06127] port-amalgams-with-a-locally-moving-factor-are-never-faithful (update): the same-action gluing of two odometer hosts is DEGENERATE (c normalizes B, cBc = B, image = B, fp, kernel killed by finitely many identification relators). - bh-outfn-2 [64d0d99d9] out-free-pip-actions-need-dimension-two-and-genuine-pieces: A BORROWED HOST MUST MATCH THE GROUP'S HONEST COORDINATES. For Out(F_n) every cheap PML substitute fails. Decisive test: a rank-3 enumeration of the projective closure.
- bh-gln-tracka [14a2c36506, 18e02cd458] z-semilocalized-embeds-in-fp-rf-group: BLIW NEEDS COFINITE DIVISIBILITY IN AN FP RF VERTEX GROUP. Every faithful cocompact locally-finite-tree host with fp edge stabilizers (BLIW Theorem A) of an input containing (Q,+) has a vertex group that is fp and RF and contains Z_(S) (S finite). Locally finite splittings cannot create such a group, and HNN letters, valence and affine self-similar scaling each supply only finitely many primes. Rings invert all primes at once but pay in K_2 (K_2(Z_(S)) maps onto the direct sum of F_q^x over q outside S). So on the tree side GL_n(Q) is exactly (SL), and (SL) is no easier than the one-prime form on the ring side. The end-shift (star-prime) route over such trees is rigid (bh-star-a) but redundant with BLIW.
- bh-free-57 (blind-commutants-of-push-families-in-ct-p-z-are-trivial, 49fa08c3d): in hosts whose germs are single affine maps, a map commuting with a self-similar push family is the identity unless the pushes read what it moves, because conjugation creates exponentially many fixed points from finitely many pieces. So any 2V in CT(Z) needs hidden, mutually-read fibre coordinates; blind writer designs are dead.
- bh-kourovka-2174a [5dee66c22] ct-z-torsion-problem-and-the-brin-thompson-transplant: SHARED SLOPE IS RIGIDITY; INDEPENDENT COORDINATES ARE COMPUTATION. Belk–Bleak get undecidable torsion in 2V from Turing machines on two independent dyadic tapes. In CT_P(Z) one rational slope acts on all p-adic coordinates, which blocks that coding: rational transfers move symbols between alphabets of coprime sizes. Undecidable in CT(Z): thin questions about single orbits (cycles, conjugacy, reaching a class). Open: thick, clopen-uniform questions (torsion = tameness, 21.74(a)). For BH, pick product-type hosts (nV, SV_G) when a compiler must run machines uniformly, and arithmetic hosts when rigidity is wanted.
- bh-g2-abh [9b2f42860] jeandel-rao-minimal-subshift-is-quantum-rigid-iff-labbe-shift-is + recognizable-morphisms-give-kakutani-equivalent-z2-groupoids: E2 IS A KAKUTANI-CLASS QUESTION. Hence ABHT's F_n×Z SFT, the Jeandel–Rao minimal subshift and Ω_U are rigid together ⟺ J_2(Ω_U)=0: SYNTHESIS items 1 and 2 are ONE computation (bh-g2-labbe). Method: build on the model that has the needed geometry (X_0 has an expansive direction for folding; Ω_U has none), and decide on the model with the smallest check (self-similar Ω_U, scale 2). Fault normals, and so the determinism no-go, are NOT invariants along non-uniform morphisms.
- bh-g1-simulation [f9ae89eb9] decidable-groups-carry-flexible-free-subshifts: on ANY decidable fg group, freeness and a decidable language come together, once minimality is dropped. Nested maximal separated markers with locally distinct IDs (4 symbols, bottom-up relay) are free, and greedy marker systems have no dead ends past a computable horizon (lemma E), so extendability is a finite check. This proves (D) and (U)+(H) (St_10(R_L) included). With the tree (A*Z gives minimality) and keyed Z² slots (finite type), E1/P1 holds for all decidable inputs at design level. Next: review lemma E; the master route's remaining gates are E2 (rigidity) and E3/P2′.
- bh-invent-17 [cocompact-tree-end-shift-hosts-are-f-infinity]: (A_N) IS FREE WITH A FINITE SHADOW BASIS. For every strongly faithful cocompact locally finite tree action with F_∞^+ edge stabilizers (BS(2,3), GBS, graphs of virtually polycyclic groups), F(G_V × (Λ⋉∂T)) is F_∞. It is a Thumann dissection operad: colours = edge orbits, transformations = edge stabilizers (infinite is fine), spine {C, T, C∨T}. So the end shift satisfies (A_N) in every degree, and E3′ holds with non-RF Λ. Isotropy enters only as transformations. The E3′ frontier is exactly infinite type, e.g. crossing-wire Z² tiles: an invariant measure rules out a finite basis, and self-similarity propagates a one-scale filling but cannot create it.

## bh-ffwz-q59 pass 3 (09-18 ~17:40): circle-type mixed identities are generated by orientation alone (7a4b5cb7a)
- **Result.** One explicit three-arc word E detects any break of cyclic order in any overgroup of homeomorphisms of the Cantor set or circle containing T. So {E} is a finite basis for T's mixed identities over all dynamical overgroups.
- **Mechanism, reusable.** In a transitive, contracting host:
  - move the defect into a fixed configuration;
  - squeeze independent conjugates into one arc;
  - read a nested commutator.
- **Lesson.** Kernel removal for circle-type Theorem C actors is not blocked by dynamics. Any NO to BFFHZ Q3.2 at T must come from abstract T-groups with no Cantor model, and the evidence points to YES.
- [bh-g2-fixedpoint-b, 755489fe5] relational-spine-couplings-are-splittable-or-not-minimal: relational freedom in seeded phases is fatal. It splits (finite phases wrap around into bumps; trees separate cones) or makes spine configurations that seeds never approach (not minimal). The functional clock over the end flow is minimal (boundary completeness: every unseeded completion is a limit of seeded ones) AND rigid (downhill). This CORRECTS my 4c2d76fe4 expectation. The live gap is input complexity (v6 CAP), not the coupling type.
- bh-emitter-b [d5cf0ee1e] a2-lattices-carry-finite-type-seeds: SEEDS OVER KAZHDAN LATTICES ARE LINK-LOCAL. The origin-projection SFT (label = simplex of Lk(x) carrying the geodesic to the origin) is a seed on every vertex-simply-transitive Ã2 lattice: projective-plane incidence forces outward labels, and descents are a confluent diamond system, which gives density. Seed idempotents are central in every quantum family, so rung 1d is purely a boundary problem. But X_Ω is a subshift of the seed, so the phase-free seed's rigidity would settle the open X_Ω claim; use the phase seed (sigma mod N, whose chamber stratum is the rigid P). Remaining: rank-one wall strata (panel-tree seeds) + gluing — a2-lattice-phase-seeds-are-quantum-rigid (open). Unconditional corollary: Gamma*Z carries a minimal topfree SFT for Kazhdan Ã2 Gamma.
- bh-free-56 [213e9e339] aanderaa-cohen-tower-subgroups-are-decidable + relative-seeds-descend-to-quotients-and-consume-membership: THE HIGMAN TOWER SEPARATES COMPLEXITY FROM GEOMETRY. (1) Consumption never blocks: every associated subgroup of the Aanderaa-Cohen tower is decidable once WP(C) and H_0(M) are (good-subgroup Britton algorithm + retraction lemma for L·P' + Nielsen change). (2) Complexity enters at ONE core-free rung, (K_M, T'_M), with membership ≡_T H_0(M) and no input group involved: CAP = rigid relative seeds for (K_M, T'_M) over hard decidable machines. (3) Geometry enters at the free factor C x F_n of H_1: gluing reaches the input only through a seed on C x F_n, so gate 2 (irreducible geometry) is forced for EVERY input by this tower, not only for (FA) inputs. (4) Relative seeds descend through normal subgroups of the stabilizer, so towers over Mihailova/fibre-product subgroups only relocate the problem into a quotient.
bh-invent-11 [5f0b62e385] seed-full-groups-act-oligomorphically-on-the-seed-orbit: ISOLATION BUYS OLIGOMORPHY. A dense orbit of isolated points puts FSym(O) inside [[Γ⋉Y]], so the full group of any seed SFT is a faithful, highly transitive actor on the seed orbit, and its orbit problem reads the seed configuration (conjugated cylinder swaps). So hard type (A) actors reduce to SEED: a hard-to-evaluate seed, an fp full group, and one fg stabilizer (hard-type-a-actors-via-seed-full-groups). Hardness can sit in y_*, not in Γ, so a CAP bound on WP(Γ) alone does not cap these actors. Γ = Z is empty, since isolated points of Z-SFTs are eventually periodic.
- bh-star-a [7e2c3693f] seed-compactifications-dominate-the-end-compactification: A SEED MUST SEE THE ENDS. You cannot glue a seed and its translate across a cut that avoids their difference set without making a second origin. So difference sets coarsely connect the seeds, and every seed compactification maps onto the Freudenthal end compactification. Over infinitely ended groups the perfect kernel maps onto a Cantor set, and rigidity must be paid there (compression). Over one-ended groups scattered locally forced seeds are rigid for free (bh-invent-10). So CAP's quantum part lives only on perfect kernels, and CAP_sc (scattered, one-ended) is purely combinatorial.
- bh-major-mcg-2 [a48b63408] THE TUBE, NOT THE PATH: bh-free-61 half-tube lemma (re-checked here) closes the totally irrational shared-ray gate in every rank ≥ 3 by a sign pigeonhole on rounding errors, with no flow recurrence or tracking needed. - [bh-free-16, 44c699ad90] dilated-registers-are-sealed: equivariance SEALS a dilated divisible register. For ANY homeomorphism t normalizing a register copy of Q as x->dx, the twist pi∘t − d·pi is locally constant (a split point of the coding forbids small twists of either sign), so the register output reads only the register input. Carries may flow OUT of a dilated register, never IN. Cross-coordinate odometer/adder germs are useless for Q x| <d> in every host.
- [bh-free-16, 44c699ad90] natural-q-rtimes-2-in-2v-h-iff-odd-odometer-roots: in product Röver–Nekrashevych hosts the dilation is free (the shift in 2V) and the primes live in the coefficient group. - bh-kourovka-2146 [ab6eeb0fd] hyperplane-extensions-into-davis-lattices-are-split: EXTENSION IS FREE, SPLITTING IS THE ENEMY. The Coxeter retraction W_L -> W_{st(D)} gives a homomorphic, commensurator-preserving section Aut(hyperplane carrier) -> Aut(X_{O_d}); Comm(W_L) = automorphisms with periodic local-action fields. So every tree-product lattice Gamma embeds through a hyperplane in a torsion-free cd-3 lattice on T x X_{O_d}. But the result is K_0 ⋊ Gamma, with K_0 acting trivially on the tree, hence never just-infinite. In lattice-built simple hosts the content is never the embedding; it is irreducibility: the gluing must be transverse to every retraction (core-free edge group). A BH host obtained by retraction-extension is always split, so simplicity needs an ingredient no retraction sees.
- [bh-invent-06b 1c0d84562] GEOMETRIC INFLATION IS A POLYNOMIAL-GROWTH PHENOMENON. A bounded-supertile hierarchy whose inflation is a QI self-embedding with expansion >1 forces polynomial growth (Franks count β(R) ≤ Mβ(R/μ), then Gromov). For regular trees, connected blocks with a tree quotient are single vertices. So no scaling tiling of the tree, F₂×F₂ or any exponential-growth group exists: the Z^d inflation cure cannot move to Track A's groups. Unlabelled compression there is fibre-type, or by Λ with isotropy (E3′). The sharp open test for free Track A: can a contracting RSG contain a free group acting freely? The product theorem (inflation host × G_V) stays undecided. Cuntz-Li-Garside, horizontal (A_N) and condition (G) each stop at fault moves, whose mcms grow in degree.
- (bh-ref-q115-b, 09-18) Label or flag categories can create SINK objects, for example the full label adj(w). The rope (infinitely many relations over a regular index set) is ONE relation transported by the free monoid on the machine digit letters; its membership is finite-state (no CAP content). Relative-seed gluing needs fg vertex groups only, so infinitely generated edge groups are harmless; reordered (digits before p) the tower is fg modulo two Britton checks. Seed-tower work concentrates in a Kurosh gluing (input-free) and the halting HNN (CAP).
- bh-g2-buildings [b17ce0c51] hyperbolic-groups-have-sft-horofunction-compactifications: (HL) PROVED. LOCAL TARGETS PROPAGATE UPSTREAM. The defect f − d(·,q) is ↓ along descents and ↑ along geodesics toward q, and hyperbolicity (four-point condition) forces every geodesic from upstream to q through q's fit window. So the defect is squeezed constant, and every hyperbolic group's horofunction compactification is an SFT with an isolated dense seed orbit. Merging/Busemann is NOT needed and fails in general (Webster–Winchester: F_3 with a redundant generator). For (★𝒞), look for this 'funnel' property plus rigidity in fp overgroups of H_Γ.
- bh-g1-universal-point [25f6d41ba] relative-seeds-on-free-groups-have-fg-stabilizers: FREE GEOMETRY FORBIDS COMPUTATION IN SEEDS. Splicing along tree edges makes every seed over a free group finite-state. Its relative seeds have f.g., regular, undistorted stabilizers, so the Higman–Clapham rope (an infinitely generated free factor) is never relative-seedable there. Undistorted edges have membership at one exponential. A CAP-refuting tower therefore needs a non-free vertex group, a non-elementarily distorted edge and a rigid relative seed forcing its coset structure (Clapham–Cohen step 4; Mikhailova fibre products).
- bh-p2b-exact [22a3770d9] v-times-gluing-presentations-are-exact: ROOM MAKES LIFTS CANONICAL. A transposition between nested/equal cones is lifted as X_0E X_0E′ X_0E through an auxiliary piece in spare room. Two auxiliaries plus the pair span a π-injective Sym(4) whose Coxeter relations involve only incomparable cones, so the lift is choice-free. Putting the auxiliary outside the conjugator kills self-swaps, which makes the lift equivariant, and the one-transposition criterion gives K = 1. So P2′b holds (exact gluing) for every group and every space; finite shadows fail only because they have no room. The group route is now (★′) ∧ P2′a: the whole remaining gate is finite presentation of LC(X,V)⋊Λ for rigid X. (lane proof, referee requested)
- (bh-ref-bs12, 09-18) Kaloujnine-Krasner embeddings land in UNRESTRICTED wreath products. - bh-invent-15 [3379f8c7c] relative-seeds-over-free-products-have-finite-ropes: across a trivial-stabilizer edge a seed transmits bounded data (branch lemma), so seeded subgroups of free groups are fg and over P*Q only finitely many cosets of Q-conjugates are seeded. - bh-e3prime [843b30d33d] kms-metabelian-layer-commensurations-have-finite-order: RANK NORMS RIGIDIFY COMMENSURATIONS; DIVISION IS THE ONLY SHRINKING LEFT. A commensuration preserves the rank of each module layer over every monomial subring. For a Baumslag–Remeslennikov counter block F_p[x^±,(1−x)^{-1}], that rank is the hexagonal norm (|q1|+|q2|+|q1+q2|)/2, and fixed-point sets recover the blocks. So every commensuration of the KMS metabelian layer permutes the counter blocks by hexagon symmetries: no homothety, no Frobenius or Cartier, no BS-type scaling. For G(M) this holds if T is preserved. The only surviving shrinking shape is division at fixed scale (lamplighter to Baumslag). That needs finite cokernels (Noetherian layers), while unbounded word-problem complexity needs non-Noetherian layers (KMS class 3). So (SS-hard)_1 needs a vertex group that is non-Noetherian where it is hard and Noetherian where it shrinks.
- [bh-free-60, d477f9e62] isolated-embedding-of-soluble-minimax-groups-via-max-n-hosts: A NOETHERIAN HOST TURNS APPROXIMATION INTO EMBEDDING. In an fp group with max-n, the maximal normal subgroup avoiding any finite set is finitely normally generated, so every finite set survives in an ISOLATED quotient. An input embeds as soon as its part invisible to finite quotients (for soluble minimax groups, the Chernikov radical, discriminated by the finite socle of its minimal invariant subgroups) is finitely discriminated; the rest is handled by residually finite, hence linear, means. So CGP Q4 for soluble minimax groups holds for all subgroups of fp max-n groups (all quotients of Abels groups) and reduces to one classical embedding question: an fp max-n overgroup.
- bh-ref-kourovka-b [21a63f2691] referee PASS on Sync_m (bh-free-61), T_{2,3}/T_tau F_infinity (bh-near-actions) and exact two-point gluing of V (bh-one-relator): LOCAL CERTIFICATES GO GLOBAL THREE WAYS. A reachability tube plus a shared vertex gives synchronization. Finite-set stabilizers plus Brown give finiteness. Natural canonical lifts plus Bleak-Quick give exactness. Each gate reduces to a check at one point, one set or one generator.
- bh-pal-kourovka61 (66c8c4292): the binary colour is a 2-cell swindle. Changing the odd-split lift by a central z moves the (2,p) defect by 2ψ(z) − z, the shadow of [B] = 2[B]. - bh-openq-lists [83b85aaed] A section-inverting endomorphism (σ(G) fixes a vertex whose section undoes σ) plants a full copy of G at a vertex of every overgroup base. - bh-kourovka-2174a [324752e8a] ct-z-monomial-elements-have-decidable-torsion: UNIFORM QUESTIONS DIE ON MULTIPLICATIVE DYNAMICS. A monomial (valuation) element is a counter machine. Its single-orbit properties (cycles, conjugacy) are undecidable, but its uniform ones (finite order = uniform periodicity) are decidable. So the step maps of a universal machine generate a group with solvable torsion problem. To embed uniform hardness, e.g. an unsolvable torsion problem, a host needs additive pieces over two independent active coordinates (Brin–Thompson type). An arithmetic host's multiplicative part provably cannot supply it.
- bh-gln-tracka [31c82140be] odometer-root-hosts-are-non-affine-and-non-contracting: A TREE STORES DIVISIBILITY ONLY IN THE ODOMETER CENTRALIZER. On the m-ary tree the l-th roots of the odometer are forced to be the m-adic translations by 1/l, and those translations have only affine normalizer. Each root is finite-state but has exponential activity, and its states have exact denominator l. So a fg self-similar group taking roots of the odometer at infinitely many primes is non-affine, non-contracting and outside Pol(inf), which excludes both known finiteness engines for self-similar groups (linear rings, contraction). The fp linear self-similar hosts (LISW Q1.11) are linear and so never contain Z_(S). The arithmetic of all primes cannot be compressed into finitely many automaton states: carrier principle, automaton form.
- bh-g1-bss (3bf21d2d6, plane-local-freedom-kills-rigidity-of-plane-lifts): the feature that made E1 easy (planes choosing floating data independently, keyed slots) is exactly what E2 forbids: plane-local freedom is a product over planes, so two far free planes kill D-rigidity (far-modification lemma), and minimality of a non-SFT lift forces free planes. E1->E2 obligation: couple floating identities across planes by bijective transports (beta-consistent keyed slots) so every modification is plane-global; then E2 = crossing-wire skeleton + crossing of copied table wires + centrality, all input-independent.
- bh-invent-13 [7f0ee9b34] ball-helly-graphs-do-not-give-the-horofunction-lemma: 'Helly' is not one property. HL needs a Helly theorem for the cells that pin EXACT distance differences (Q(e,k) = {q : d(u,q) − d(v,q) = k}). Those cells are halfspaces in median and H² geometries, and there Helly works. Ball-Helly bounds distances only from above, which the triangle inequality already gives (a factor-2 coarse gain at most, never exactness), and in the king's graph the cells are not even convex. So CCGHO's 'every hyperbolic group is Helly' (arXiv 2002.06895, Thm 1.1) transfers nothing to HL. The tube lemma stays the route for Kazhdan hyperbolic groups, and C̃_n lattices (Helly and Kazhdan) are the calibration for R-pattern-cell Helly.
- [bh-invent-02, 084a4cff6] class-wise-increasing-rcwa-p-groups-are-abelian-cofinite-hosts: compare a glued image with the FULL GROUP OF THE GENERATED GROUPOID, not its N_0-stable part. CT_P(Z) misses the translation (infinite index); R_P = F(Lambda_P x| Z) (odometer on the class-transposition k-graph, no extra colour) is F_inf by Li ex:ZS(III) (thm:deg check for Lambda_P alone pending) and is itself a cyclically cofinite host on Z. Kernel removal for groupoid-coded factors = 'do the factors' full groups generate the full group of the generated groupoid?'; for B *_<a> B_3 this is G_(2,3) = R_(3), true mod 6. For bh-pal-kourovka61 / bh-free-57: is <V_(2,1)(a), V_(3,1)(a)> = R_(3)?
- bh-invent-04 (b12f837af, busemann-transplant-minimality-is-horospherical-minimality): TRANSPLANT MINIMALITY = HOROSPHERICAL MINIMALITY of the skeleton (every horosphere sees every germ). The only obstruction is arithmetic: a Busemann phase must avoid the fibre's finite factors, so their moduli must be coprime. Rigidity, freeness, minimality and non-amenability are then four independent layers. F_2×F_2 gets a free minimal quantum-rigid SFT (gate G2(c)), conditional on the minimal one-way crossing-wire fibre. Ã2 Kazhdan lattices get the same, up to one finite 2-graph primitivity check.
- bh-invent-10 [d15e53b53] baumslag-gersten-groups-lie-in-class-c: TOWER RIGIDITY IS PAID ON THE BOUNDARY, WHERE SINK DATA MUST COLLAPSE TO FINITELY MANY GLOBAL PARAMETERS. The line-sink relative seed for (BS(1,n), <b>) had halving-coupled C_Z counters.
